函数名: arraylist_new
生成代码:
pub fn arraylist_new<T>(length: u32) -> Option<ArrayList<T>> {
    let mut length = length;
    if length == 0 {
        length = 16;
    }
    let new_arraylist = ArrayList {
        data: Vec::with_capacity(length as usize),
        length: 0,
        _alloced: length,
    };
    Some(new_arraylist)
}
标准答案:
pub fn arraylist_new<T>(mut length: u32) -> Option<ArrayList<T>> {
    if length <= 0 {
        length = 16;
    }
    let mut new_arraylist = ArrayList::new();
    
    new_arraylist._alloced = length;
    new_arraylist.length = 0;
    new_arraylist.data.resize_with(length as usize, || None);
    Some(new_arraylist)
}

pub fn arraylist_free<T>(arraylist: &mut ArrayList<T>) {
    if arraylist.is_some() {
        arraylist.data.clear();
        arraylist.take();
    }
}

函数名: arraylist_enlarge
生成代码:
pub fn arraylist_enlarge<T>(arraylist: &mut ArrayList<T>) -> i32 {
    let newsize = arraylist._alloced * 2;
    let mut data = std::mem::take(&mut arraylist.data);
    data.resize_with(newsize as usize, || None);
    if data.is_empty() {
        return 0;
    } else {
        arraylist.data = data;
        arraylist._alloced = newsize;
        return 1;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn arraylist_enlarge<T>(arraylist: &mut ArrayList<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut data: Vec<ArrayListValue<T>>;
    let mut newsize: u32;

    newsize = arraylist._alloced * 2;
	data = std::mem::take(&mut arraylist.data);
	data.resize_with(newsize as usize, || None);
	arraylist.data = data;
	arraylist._alloced = newsize;
	return 1;
}

函数名: arraylist_insert
生成代码:
pub fn arraylist_insert<T>(arraylist: &mut ArrayList<T>, index: u32, data: ArrayListValue<T>) -> i32 {
    if index > arraylist.length {
        return 0;
    }
    if arraylist.length + 1 > arraylist._alloced {
        if arraylist_enlarge(arraylist) == 0 {
            return 0;
        }
    }
    let len = arraylist.length as usize;
    let idx = index as usize;
    arraylist.data.copy_within(idx..len, idx + 1);
    arraylist.data[idx] = data;
    arraylist.length += 1;
    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn arraylist_insert<T>(arraylist: &mut ArrayList<T>, index: u32, data: ArrayListValue<T>) -> i32 {
	if index > arraylist.length {
		return 0;
	}

	if arraylist.length + 1 > arraylist._alloced {
		if arraylist_enlarge(arraylist) == 0 {
			return 0;
		}
	}

    for i in (index..arraylist.length).rev() {
        arraylist.data[(i + 1) as usize] = arraylist.data[i as usize].take();
    }

	arraylist.data[index as usize] = data;
	arraylist.length += 1;
	return 1;
}

函数名: arraylist_remove_range
生成代码:
pub fn arraylist_remove_range<T>(arraylist: &mut ArrayList<T>, index: u32, length: u32) {
    if index >= arraylist.length || index + length > arraylist.length {
        return;
    }
    let src_start = (index + length) as usize;
    let dst_start = index as usize;
    let count = (arraylist.length - (index + length)) as usize;
    arraylist.data.copy_within(src_start..(src_start + count), dst_start);
    arraylist.length -= length;
}
标准答案:
pub fn arraylist_remove_range<T>(arraylist: &mut ArrayList<T>, index: u32, length: u32) {
	if index > arraylist.length || index + length > arraylist.length {
		return;
	}
    for i in 0..arraylist.length - index - length {
        arraylist.data[(index + i) as usize] = arraylist.data[(index + length + i) as usize].take();
    }
	arraylist.length -= length;
}

函数名: arraylist_index_of
生成代码:
pub fn arraylist_index_of<T>(arraylist: &ArrayList<T>, callback: ArrayListEqualFunc<T>, data: ArrayListValue<T>) -> i32 {
    for i in 0..arraylist.length {
        if callback(arraylist.data[i as usize], data) {
            return i as i32;
        }
    }
    -1
}
标准答案:
pub fn arraylist_index_of<T>(arraylist: &mut ArrayList<T>, callback: ArrayListEqualFunc<T>, data: &mut ArrayListValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut i: u32;
    cfor!(i = 0; i < arraylist.length; i += 1; {
        if callback(&arraylist.data[i as usize], &data) != 0 {
            return i as i32;
        }
    });
	return -1;
}

函数名: arraylist_sort_internal
生成代码:
pub fn arraylist_sort_internal<T>(list_data: &mut [ArrayListValue<T>], list_length: u32, compare_func: ArrayListCompareFunc<T>) {
    if list_length <= 1 {
        return;
    }

    let pivot = list_data[(list_length - 1) as usize];
    let mut list1_length = 0;

    for i in 0..(list_length - 1) {
        if compare_func(list_data[i as usize], pivot) < 0 {
            list_data.swap(i as usize, list1_length as usize);
            list1_length += 1;
        }
    }

    let list2_length = list_length - list1_length - 1;
    list_data.swap((list_length - 1) as usize, list1_length as usize);

    arraylist_sort_internal(list_data, list1_length, compare_func);
    arraylist_sort_internal(&mut list_data[(list1_length + 1) as usize..], list2_length, compare_func);
}
标准答案:
pub fn arraylist_sort_internal<T>(list_data: &mut [ArrayListValue<T>], list_length: u32, compare_func: ArrayListCompareFunc<T>) {
	let mut pivot: ArrayListValue<T>;
	let mut tmp: ArrayListValue<T>;
	let mut i: u32;
	let mut list1_length: u32;
	let mut list2_length: u32;

	if list_length <= 1 {
		return;
	}

    pivot = list_data[list_length as usize - 1].take();

	list1_length = 0;

	for i in 0..list_length - 1 {
		if compare_func(&list_data[i as usize], &pivot) < 0 {
            let tmp = list_data[i as usize].take();
            list_data[i as usize] = list_data[list1_length as usize].take();
            list_data[list1_length as usize] = tmp;
			list1_length += 1;
		}
	}

	list2_length = list_length - list1_length - 1;
    list_data[list_length as usize - 1] = list_data[list1_length as usize].take();
	list_data[list1_length as usize] = pivot;

	arraylist_sort_internal(&mut list_data[0..list1_length as usize], list1_length, compare_func);
	arraylist_sort_internal(&mut list_data[(list1_length + 1) as usize..], list2_length, compare_func);
}

}

pub fn avl_tree_new<V>(compare_func: AVLTreeCompareFunc<V>) -> Option<AVLTree<(), V>> {
    let new_tree = Box::new(AVLTree::new(compare_func));
    Some(*new_tree)
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_new<K, V>(compare_func: AVLTreeCompareFunc<K>) -> Option<AVLTree<K, V>> {
    let mut new_tree: AVLTree<K, V>;
    new_tree = AVLTree::new();
    new_tree.compare_func = compare_func;
    new_tree.num_nodes = 0;
    return Some(new_tree);
}

函数名: avl_tree_free_subtree
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_free_subtree<K, V>(tree: &AVLTree<K, V>, node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    if node.is_none() {
        return;
    }
    let node_ref = node.unwrap();
    avl_tree_free_subtree(tree, node_ref.borrow().children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize].clone());
    avl_tree_free_subtree(tree, node_ref.borrow().children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize].clone());
    drop(node_ref);
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_free_subtree<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, mut node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    if node.is_none() {
        return;
    }
    avl_tree_free_subtree(tree, node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_left!()]);
    avl_tree_free_subtree(tree, node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_right!()]);
    node.drop();
}

    return node.borrow().height;

函数名: avl_tree_update_height
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_update_height<K, V>(node: &mut AVLTreeNode<K, V>) {
    let left_subtree = &node.children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize];
    let right_subtree = &node.children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize];
    let left_height = avl_tree_subtree_height(left_subtree.clone());
    let right_height = avl_tree_subtree_height(right_subtree.clone());
    if left_height > right_height {
        node.height = left_height + 1;
    } else {
        node.height = right_height + 1;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_update_height<K, V>(mut node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    let mut left_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut right_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut left_height: i32;
    let mut right_height: i32;

    left_subtree = node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_left!()];
    right_subtree = node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_right!()];
    left_height = avl_tree_subtree_height(left_subtree);
    right_height = avl_tree_subtree_height(right_subtree);

    if left_height > right_height {
        node.borrow_mut().height = left_height + 1;
    } else {
        node.borrow_mut().height = right_height + 1;
    }
}

函数名: avl_tree_node_parent_side
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_node_parent_side(node: &AVLTreeNode<K, V>) -> AVLTreeNodeSide {
    if node.parent.as_ref().unwrap().children[AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize].as_ref() == Some(node) {
        return AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT;
    } else {
        return AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_node_parent_side<K, V>(mut node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) -> AVLTreeNodeSide {
    if node.borrow().parent.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_left!()] == node {
        return avl_tree_node_left!();
    } else {
        return avl_tree_node_right!();
    }
}

函数名: avl_tree_node_replace
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_node_replace<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, node1: &mut AVLTreeNode<K, V>, node2: Option<Box<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>>) {
    if let Some(node2_ref) = node2 {
        node2_ref.parent = node1.parent.take();
    }
    if node1.parent.is_none() {
        tree.root_node = node2;
    } else {
        let side = avl_tree_node_parent_side(node1);
        if let Some(parent) = node1.parent.as_mut() {
            parent.children[side as usize] = node2;
            avl_tree_update_height(parent);
        }
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_node_replace<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, mut node1: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>, mut node2: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    let mut side: AVLTreeNodeSide;
    if node2.is_some() {
        node2.borrow_mut().parent = node1.borrow().parent;
    }
    if node1.borrow().parent.is_none() {
        tree.root_node = node2;
    } else {
        side = avl_tree_node_parent_side(node1);
        node1.borrow_mut().parent.borrow_mut().children[side as usize] = node2;
        avl_tree_update_height(node1.borrow().parent);
    }
}

函数名: avl_tree_rotate
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_rotate<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, node: &mut AVLTreeNode<K, V>, direction: AVLTreeNodeSide) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let new_root = node.children[1 - direction as usize].take();
    avl_tree_node_replace(tree, node, new_root.as_mut().map(|n| n.as_mut()));
    node.children[1 - direction as usize] = new_root.as_ref().and_then(|n| n.children[direction as usize].take());
    new_root.as_mut().unwrap().children[direction as usize] = Some(Box::new(node.clone()));
    node.parent = new_root.as_ref().map(|n| n.as_ref());
    if let Some(child) = node.children[1 - direction as usize].as_mut() {
        child.parent = Some(Box::new(node.clone()));
    }
    avl_tree_update_height(new_root.as_mut().unwrap());
    avl_tree_update_height(node);
    new_root
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_rotate<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, mut node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>, direction: AVLTreeNodeSide) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut new_root: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    new_root = node.borrow().children[1 - direction as usize];
    avl_tree_node_replace(tree, node, new_root);
    node.borrow_mut().children[1 - direction as usize] = new_root.borrow().children[direction as usize];
    new_root.borrow_mut().children[direction as usize] = node;
    node.borrow_mut().parent = new_root;    
    if node.borrow().children[1 - direction as usize].is_some() {
        node.borrow_mut().children[1 - direction as usize].borrow_mut().parent = node;
    }
    avl_tree_update_height(node);
    avl_tree_update_height(new_root);
    
    return new_root;
}

函数名: avl_tree_node_balance
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_node_balance<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, node: &mut AVLTreeNode<K, V>) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let left_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let right_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut child: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut diff: i32;

    left_subtree = node.children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize].clone();
    right_subtree = node.children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize].clone();

    diff = avl_tree_subtree_height(right_subtree) - avl_tree_subtree_height(left_subtree);

    if diff >= 2 {
        child = right_subtree;
        if avl_tree_subtree_height(child.as_ref().unwrap().children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize].clone())
            < avl_tree_subtree_height(child.as_ref().unwrap().children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize].clone()) {
            avl_tree_rotate(tree, child.as_mut().unwrap(), AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT);
        }
        node = avl_tree_rotate(tree, node, AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT).as_mut().unwrap();
    } else if diff <= -2 {
        child = left_subtree;
        if avl_tree_subtree_height(child.as_ref().unwrap().children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize].clone())
            < avl_tree_subtree_height(child.as_ref().unwrap().children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize].clone()) {
            avl_tree_rotate(tree, child.as_mut().unwrap(), AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT);
        }
        node = avl_tree_rotate(tree, node, AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT).as_mut().unwrap();
    }

    avl_tree_update_height(node);
    Some(node.clone())
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_node_balance<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, mut node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut left_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut right_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut child: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut diff: i32; 

    left_subtree = node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_left!()];
    right_subtree = node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_right!()];
    diff = avl_tree_subtree_height(right_subtree) - avl_tree_subtree_height(left_subtree);

    if diff >= 2 {
        child = right_subtree;
        if avl_tree_subtree_height(child.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_right!()]) < avl_tree_subtree_height(child.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_left!()]) {
            avl_tree_rotate(tree, right_subtree, avl_tree_node_right!());
        }
        node = avl_tree_rotate(tree, node, avl_tree_node_left!());
    } else if diff <= -2 {
        child = node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_left!()];
        if avl_tree_subtree_height(child.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_left!()]) < avl_tree_subtree_height(child.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_right!()]) {
            avl_tree_rotate(tree, left_subtree, avl_tree_node_left!());
        }
        node = avl_tree_rotate(tree, node, avl_tree_node_right!());
    }
    avl_tree_update_height(node);
    return node;
}

函数名: avl_tree_balance_to_root
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_balance_to_root<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    let mut rover = node;
    while rover.is_some() {
        rover = avl_tree_node_balance(tree, rover.as_mut().unwrap());
        rover = rover.unwrap().parent;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_balance_to_root<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    let mut rover: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    rover = node;
    while rover.is_some() {
        rover = avl_tree_node_balance(tree, rover);
        rover = rover.borrow().parent;
    }
}

函数名: avl_tree_insert
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_insert<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, key: AVLTreeKey<K>, value: AVLTreeValue<V>) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut rover: &mut Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> = &mut tree.root_node;
    let mut previous_node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> = None;

    while rover.is_some() {
        previous_node = rover.take();
        if (tree.compare_func)(&key, &rover.as_ref().unwrap().key) < 0 {
            rover = &mut rover.as_mut().unwrap().children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize];
        } else {
            rover = &mut rover.as_mut().unwrap().children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize];
        }
    }

    let new_node = Box::new(AVLTreeNode::new());
    let new_node_ptr = Some(new_node);

    if new_node_ptr.is_none() {
        return None;
    }

    let new_node_ref = new_node_ptr.as_mut().unwrap();
    new_node_ref.children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize] = None;
    new_node_ref.children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize] = None;
    new_node_ref.parent = previous_node;
    new_node_ref.key = key;
    new_node_ref.value = value;
    new_node_ref.height = 1;

    *rover = new_node_ptr;

    avl_tree_balance_to_root(tree, previous_node);

    tree.num_nodes += 1;

    return new_node_ptr;
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_insert<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, mut key: AVLTreeKey<K>, value: AVLTreeValue<V>) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut rover: LinkRover<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut new_node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut previous_node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;

    rover = tree.root_node.rover();
    previous_node = None;

    while rover.is_some() {
        previous_node = *rover;
        if (tree.compare_func)(&key, &rover.borrow().key) < 0 {
            rover = rover.borrow_mut().children[avl_tree_node_left!()].rover();
        } 
        else {
            rover = rover.borrow_mut().children[avl_tree_node_right!()].rover();
        }
    }

    let mut new_node = Link::new(AVLTreeNode::new());
    new_node.borrow_mut().parent = previous_node;
    new_node.borrow_mut().key = key;
    new_node.borrow_mut().value = value;
    new_node.borrow_mut().height = 1;
    *rover = new_node;
    avl_tree_balance_to_root(tree, previous_node);
    tree.num_nodes += 1;
    return new_node;
}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

函数名: avl_tree_node_get_replacement
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_node_get_replacement<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, node: &mut AVLTreeNode<K, V>) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let left_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> = node.children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize].clone();
    let right_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> = node.children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize].clone();
    let mut result: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut child: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut left_height: i32;
    let mut right_height: i32;
    let mut side: usize;

    if left_subtree.is_none() && right_subtree.is_none() {
        return None;
    }

    left_height = avl_tree_subtree_height(left_subtree);
    right_height = avl_tree_subtree_height(right_subtree);

    if left_height < right_height {
        side = AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize;
    } else {
        side = AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize;
    }

    result = node.children[side].clone();

    while result.as_ref().unwrap().children[1 - side].is_some() {
        result = result.unwrap().children[1 - side].clone();
    }

    child = result.as_ref().unwrap().children[side].clone();
    avl_tree_node_replace(tree, result.as_mut().unwrap(), child);
    avl_tree_update_height(result.as_mut().unwrap().parent.as_mut().unwrap());

    result
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_node_get_replacement<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, mut node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut left_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut right_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut result: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut child: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut left_height: i32;
    let mut right_height: i32;
    let mut side: AVLTreeNodeSide;

    left_subtree = node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_left!()];
    right_subtree = node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_right!()];

    if left_subtree.is_none() && right_subtree.is_none() {
        return None;
    }

    left_height = avl_tree_subtree_height(left_subtree);
    right_height = avl_tree_subtree_height(right_subtree);

    if left_height < right_height {
        side = avl_tree_node_right!();
    } else {
        side = avl_tree_node_left!();
    }

    result = node.borrow().children[side as usize];

    while result.borrow().children[1 - side as usize].is_some() {
        result = result.borrow().children[1 - side as usize];
    }

    child = result.borrow().children[side as usize];
    avl_tree_node_replace(tree, result, child);
    avl_tree_update_height(result);
    return result;    
}

函数名: avl_tree_remove_node
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_remove_node<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, node: &mut AVLTreeNode<K, V>) {
    let mut swap_node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut balance_startpoint: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut i: usize;

    swap_node = avl_tree_node_get_replacement(tree, node);
    if swap_node.is_none() {
        avl_tree_node_replace(tree, node, None);
        balance_startpoint = node.parent.clone();
    } else {
        if swap_node.as_ref().unwrap().parent.as_ref().map_or(false, |p| std::ptr::eq(p, node)) {
            balance_startpoint = swap_node.clone();
        } else {
            balance_startpoint = swap_node.as_ref().unwrap().parent.clone();
        }
        for i in 0..2 {
            swap_node.as_mut().unwrap().children[i] = node.children[i].clone();
            if swap_node.as_ref().unwrap().children[i].is_some() {
                swap_node.as_mut().unwrap().children[i].as_mut().unwrap().parent = swap_node.clone();
            }
        }
        swap_node.as_mut().unwrap().height = node.height;
        avl_tree_node_replace(tree, node, Some(Box::new(swap_node.unwrap())));
    }
    drop(node);
    tree.num_nodes -= 1;
    avl_tree_balance_to_root(tree, balance_startpoint);
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_remove_node<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, mut node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    let mut swap_node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut balance_startpoint: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut i: i32;
    swap_node = avl_tree_node_get_replacement(tree, node);
    if swap_node.is_none() {
        avl_tree_node_replace(tree, node, None);
        balance_startpoint = node.borrow().parent;
    } else {
        if swap_node.eq(&node) {
            balance_startpoint = swap_node;
        } else {
            balance_startpoint = swap_node.borrow().parent;
        }
        cfor!(i = 0; i < 2; i += 1; {
            swap_node.borrow_mut().children[i as usize] = node.borrow().children[i as usize];
            if swap_node.borrow().children[i as usize].is_some() {
                swap_node.borrow_mut().children[i as usize].borrow_mut().parent = swap_node;
            }
        });
        swap_node.borrow_mut().height = node.borrow().height;
        avl_tree_node_replace(tree, node, swap_node);
    }
    node.drop();
    tree.num_nodes -= 1;
    avl_tree_balance_to_root(tree, balance_startpoint);
}

函数名: avl_tree_lookup_node
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_lookup_node<K, V>(tree: &AVLTree<K, V>, key: AVLTreeKey<K>) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut node = tree.root_node.clone();
    while let Some(n) = node {
        let diff = (tree.compare_func)(key.clone(), n.borrow().key.clone());
        if diff == 0 {
            return Some(n);
        } else if diff < 0 {
            node = n.borrow().children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize].clone();
        } else {
            node = n.borrow().children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize].clone();
        }
    }
    None
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_lookup_node<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, key: &mut AVLTreeKey<K>) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut diff: i32;

    node = tree.root_node;

    while node.is_some() {
        diff = (tree.compare_func)(&key, &node.borrow().key);

        if diff == 0 {
            return node;
        } else if diff < 0 {
            node = node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_left!()];
        } else {
            node = node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_right!()];
        }
    }

    return None;
}

pub fn avl_tree_remove<K, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>, key: AVLTreeKey<K>) -> i32 {

    if node.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    avl_tree_remove_node(tree, node.as_mut().unwrap());
    return 1;
}

    node = avl_tree_lookup_node(tree, key);
    if node.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    avl_tree_remove_node(tree, node);
    return 1;
}

函数名: avl_tree_node_child
生成代码:
pub fn avl_tree_node_child<K, V>(node: &Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>, side: AVLTreeNodeSide) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    match side {
        AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT => node.as_ref().map(|n| &n.children[0]).unwrap_or(&None).clone(),
        AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT => node.as_ref().map(|n| &n.children[1]).unwrap_or(&None).clone(),
        _ => None,
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_node_child<K, V>(mut node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>, side: AVLTreeNodeSide) -> Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>> {
    if side == avl_tree_node_left!() {
        return node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_left!()];
    } else if side == avl_tree_node_right!() {
        return node.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_right!()];
    } else {
        return None;
    }
}

pub fn avl_tree_to_array_add_subtree<K, V>(subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>, array: &mut [AVLTreeValue<K>], index: &mut usize) {
    if subtree.is_none() {
        return;
    }
    let subtree_node = subtree.as_ref().unwrap();
    avl_tree_to_array_add_subtree(subtree_node.children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize].clone(), array, index);
    array[*index] = subtree_node.key.clone();
    *index += 1;
    avl_tree_to_array_add_subtree(subtree_node.children[AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize].clone(), array, index);
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_to_array_add_subtree<K: Clone, V>(mut subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>, array: &mut [Option<K>], index: &mut i32) {
    if subtree.is_none() {
        return;
    }
    avl_tree_to_array_add_subtree(subtree.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_left!()], array, index);
    array[*index as usize] = subtree.borrow_mut().key.clone();
    *index += 1;
    avl_tree_to_array_add_subtree(subtree.borrow().children[avl_tree_node_right!()], array, index);
}

pub fn avl_tree_to_array<K, V>(tree: &AVLTree<K, V>) -> Option<Vec<AVLTreeValue<V>>> {
    let mut array: Vec<AVLTreeValue<V>> = Vec::with_capacity(tree.num_nodes as usize);
    if array.is_none() {
        return None;
    }
    let mut index: usize = 0;
    avl_tree_to_array_add_subtree(tree.root_node, &mut array, &mut index);
    Some(array)
}
标准答案:
pub fn avl_tree_to_array<K: Clone, V>(tree: &mut AVLTree<K, V>) -> Vec<Option<K>> {
    let mut array: Vec<Option<K>>;
    let mut index: i32;
    array = vec![None; tree.num_nodes as usize];
    index = 0;
    avl_tree_to_array_add_subtree(tree.root_node, &mut array, &mut index);
    return array;
}

pub fn binary_heap_cmp<T>(heap: &BinaryHeap<T>, data1: BinaryHeapValue<T>, data2: BinaryHeapValue<T>) -> i32 {
    match heap.heap_type {
        BinaryHeapType::BINARY_HEAP_TYPE_MIN => (heap.compare_func)(data1, data2),
        BinaryHeapType::BINARY_HEAP_TYPE_MAX => -(heap.compare_func)(data1, data2),
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn binary_heap_cmp<T>(heap: &BinaryHeap<T>, data1: &BinaryHeapValue<T>, data2: &BinaryHeapValue<T>) -> i32 {
    if heap.heap_type == binary_heap_type_min!() {
        return (heap.compare_func)(&data1, &data2);
    } else {
        return -(heap.compare_func)(&data1, &data2);
    }
}

pub fn binary_heap_new<T>(heap_type: BinaryHeapType, compare_func: BinaryHeapCompareFunc<T>) -> Option<BinaryHeap<T>> {
    let heap = BinaryHeap {
        heap_type,
        values: Vec::with_capacity(16),
        num_values: 0,
        alloced_size: 16,
        compare_func,
    };

    Some(heap)
}
标准答案:
pub fn binary_heap_new<T>(heap_type: BinaryHeapType, compare_func: BinaryHeapCompareFunc<T>) -> Option<BinaryHeap<T>> {
    let mut heap: BinaryHeap<T>;
    heap = BinaryHeap::new();
    heap.heap_type = heap_type;
    heap.num_values = 0;
    heap.compare_func = compare_func;

    heap.alloced_size = 16;
    heap.values.resize_with(heap.alloced_size as usize, || None);
    Some(heap)
}

            heap_ref.values.clear();
        }
        std::mem::drop(heap);
    }

函数名: binary_heap_insert
生成代码:
pub fn binary_heap_insert<T>(heap: &mut BinaryHeap<T>, value: BinaryHeapValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut new_values: Vec<BinaryHeapValue<T>>;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut new_size: u32;
    let mut parent: u32;

    if heap.num_values >= heap.alloced_size {
        new_size = heap.alloced_size * 2;
        new_values = heap.values.resize(new_size as usize, BINARY_HEAP_NULL);
        if new_values.is_none() {
            return 0;
        }
        heap.alloced_size = new_size;
        heap.values = new_values.unwrap();
    }

    index = heap.num_values;
    heap.num_values += 1;

    while index > 0 {
        parent = (index - 1) / 2;
        if (heap.compare_func)(heap.values[parent as usize], value) < 0 {
            break;
        } else {
            heap.values[index as usize] = heap.values[parent as usize];
            index = parent;
        }
    }

    heap.values[index as usize] = value;
    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn binary_heap_insert<T>(heap: &mut BinaryHeap<T>, value: BinaryHeapValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut new_values: Vec<BinaryHeapValue<T>>;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut new_size: u32;
    let mut parent: u32;
    if heap.num_values >= heap.alloced_size {
        new_size = heap.alloced_size * 2;
        new_values = std::mem::take(&mut heap.values);
        new_values.resize_with(new_size as usize, || None);
        heap.alloced_size = new_size;
        heap.values = new_values;
    }
    index = heap.num_values;
    heap.num_values += 1;
    while index > 0 {
        parent = (index - 1) / 2;
        if binary_heap_cmp(heap, &heap.values[parent as usize], &value) < 0 {
            break;
        } else {
            heap.values[index as usize] = heap.values[parent as usize].take();
            index = parent;
        }
    }
    heap.values[index as usize] = value;
    return 1;
}

函数名: binary_heap_pop
生成代码:
pub fn binary_heap_pop<T>(heap: &mut BinaryHeap<T>) -> BinaryHeapValue<T> {
    if heap.num_values == 0 {
        return BINARY_HEAP_NULL;
    }

    let result = heap.values[0].take();
    let new_value = heap.values[heap.num_values as usize - 1].take();
    heap.num_values -= 1;

    let mut index = 0;
    loop {
        let child1 = index * 2 + 1;
        let child2 = index * 2 + 2;

        if child1 < heap.num_values as usize && (heap.compare_func)(new_value.clone(), heap.values[child1].clone()) > 0 {
            if child2 < heap.num_values as usize && (heap.compare_func)(heap.values[child1].clone(), heap.values[child2].clone()) > 0 {
                heap.values[index] = heap.values[child2].take();
                index = child2;
            } else {
                heap.values[index] = heap.values[child1].take();
                index = child1;
            }
        } else if child2 < heap.num_values as usize && (heap.compare_func)(new_value.clone(), heap.values[child2].clone()) > 0 {
            heap.values[index] = heap.values[child2].take();
            index = child2;
        } else {
            heap.values[index] = new_value;
            break;
        }
    }

    result
}
标准答案:
pub fn binary_heap_pop<T>(heap: &mut BinaryHeap<T>) -> BinaryHeapValue<T> {
    let mut result: BinaryHeapValue<T>;
    let mut new_value: BinaryHeapValue<T>;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut next_index: u32;
    let mut child1: u32;
    let mut child2: u32;

    if heap.num_values == 0 {
        return binary_heap_null!();
    }
    result = heap.values[0].take();

    new_value = heap.values[(heap.num_values - 1) as usize].take();
    heap.num_values -= 1;

    index = 0;
    loop {
        child1 = index * 2 + 1;
        child2 = index * 2 + 2;
        if child1 < heap.num_values && binary_heap_cmp(heap, &new_value, &heap.values[child1 as usize]) > 0 {
            if child2 < heap.num_values && binary_heap_cmp(heap, &heap.values[child1 as usize], &heap.values[child2 as usize]) > 0 {
                next_index = child2;
            } else {
                next_index = child1;
            }

        } else if child2 < heap.num_values
                && binary_heap_cmp(heap, &new_value, &heap.values[child2 as usize]) > 0 {
            next_index = child2;

        } else {
            heap.values[index as usize] = new_value;
            break;
        }
        heap.values[index as usize] = heap.values[next_index as usize].take();
        index = next_index;
    }
    return result;
}

函数名: binomial_tree_unref
生成代码:
pub fn binomial_tree_unref<T>(tree: Option<&mut BinomialTree<T>>) {
    if let Some(tree) = tree {
        tree.refcount -= 1;
        if tree.refcount == 0 {
            for i in 0..tree.order {
                binomial_tree_unref(tree.subtrees[i as usize].as_mut());
            }
            tree.subtrees.clear();
        }
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn binomial_tree_unref<T>(mut tree: Link<BinomialTree<T>>) {
    let mut i: i32;
    if tree.is_none() {
        return;
    }

    tree.borrow_mut().refcount -= 1;

    if tree.borrow().refcount == 0 {
        cfor!(i = 0; i < tree.borrow().order as i32; i += 1; {
            binomial_tree_unref(tree.borrow().subtrees[i as usize]);
        });
        tree.drop();
    }
}

函数名: binomial_tree_merge
生成代码:
pub fn binomial_tree_merge<T>(heap: &BinomialHeap<T>, tree1: &mut Link<BinomialTree<T>>, tree2: &mut Link<BinomialTree<T>>) -> Option<Link<BinomialTree<T>>> {
    let mut new_tree: Link<BinomialTree<T>>;
    let mut tmp: Link<BinomialTree<T>>;
    let mut i: usize;

    if binomial_heap_cmp(heap, tree1.as_ref().unwrap().value, tree2.as_ref().unwrap().value) > 0 {
        tmp = tree1.take();
        *tree1 = tree2.take();
        *tree2 = tmp;
    }

    new_tree = Some(Box::new(BinomialTree::new()));
    if new_tree.is_none() {
        return None;
    }

    let new_tree_ref = new_tree.as_mut().unwrap();
    new_tree_ref.refcount = 0;
    new_tree_ref.order = tree1.as_ref().unwrap().order + 1;
    new_tree_ref.value = tree1.as_ref().unwrap().value.clone();
    new_tree_ref.subtrees = Vec::with_capacity(new_tree_ref.order as usize);

    if new_tree_ref.subtrees.is_none() {
        return None;
    }

    new_tree_ref.subtrees.extend_from_slice(&tree1.as_ref().unwrap().subtrees);
    new_tree_ref.subtrees[new_tree_ref.order as usize - 1] = tree2.take();

    for i in 0..new_tree_ref.order as usize {
        binomial_tree_ref(&mut new_tree_ref.subtrees[i]);
    }

    Some(new_tree)
}
标准答案:
pub fn binomial_tree_merge<T>(heap: &mut BinomialHeap<T>, mut tree1: Link<BinomialTree<T>>, mut tree2: Link<BinomialTree<T>>) -> Link<BinomialTree<T>> {
    let mut new_tree: Link<BinomialTree<T>>;
    let mut tmp: Link<BinomialTree<T>>;
    let mut i: i32;

    if binomial_heap_cmp(heap, &tree1.borrow().value, &tree2.borrow().value) > 0 {
        tmp = tree1;
        tree1 = tree2;
        tree2 = tmp;
    }
    let mut new_tree = Link::new(BinomialTree::new());
    new_tree.borrow_mut().refcount = 0;
    new_tree.borrow_mut().order = tree1.borrow().order + 1;
    new_tree.borrow_mut().value = tree1.borrow_mut().value.take();
    new_tree.borrow_mut().subtrees = vec![None; new_tree.borrow().order as usize];
    for i in 0..tree1.borrow().order {
        new_tree.borrow_mut().subtrees[i as usize] = tree1.borrow().subtrees[i as usize];
    }
    let order = new_tree.borrow().order;
    new_tree.borrow_mut().subtrees[order as usize - 1] = tree2;
    cfor!(i = 0; i < new_tree.borrow().order as i32; i += 1; {
        binomial_tree_ref(new_tree.borrow_mut().subtrees[i as usize]);
    });
    return new_tree;
}

pub fn binomial_heap_merge_undo<T>(new_roots: &mut Vec<Link<BinomialTree<T>>>, count: usize) {
    for i in 0..=count {
        binomial_tree_unref(new_roots[i].as_mut());
    }
    new_roots.clear();
}

    let mut i: i32;
    cfor!(i = 0; i < new_roots.len() as i32; i += 1; {
        binomial_tree_unref(new_roots[i as usize]);
    });
}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

函数名: binomial_heap_merge
生成代码:
pub fn binomial_heap_merge<T>(heap: &mut BinomialHeap<T>, other: &mut BinomialHeap<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut new_roots: Vec<Link<BinomialTree<T>>>;
    let mut new_roots_length: usize;
    let mut vals: [Option<Link<BinomialTree<T>>>; 3];
    let mut num_vals: usize;
    let mut carry: Option<Link<BinomialTree<T>>>;
    let mut new_carry: Option<Link<BinomialTree<T>>>;
    let max: usize;
    let mut i: usize;

    if heap.roots_length > other.roots_length {
        max = heap.roots_length as usize + 1;
    } else {
        max = other.roots_length as usize + 1;
    }

    new_roots = vec![None; max];
    new_roots_length = 0;
    carry = None;

    for i in 0..max {
        num_vals = 0;
        if i < heap.roots_length as usize && heap.roots[i].is_some() {
            vals[num_vals] = heap.roots[i].clone();
            num_vals += 1;
        }
        if i < other.roots_length as usize && other.roots[i].is_some() {
            vals[num_vals] = other.roots[i].clone();
            num_vals += 1;
        }
        if carry.is_some() {
            vals[num_vals] = carry.clone();
            num_vals += 1;
        }

        if num_vals & 1 != 0 {
            new_roots[i] = vals[num_vals - 1].clone();
            binomial_tree_ref(&mut new_roots[i]);
            new_roots_length = i + 1;
        } else {
            new_roots[i] = None;
        }

        if num_vals & 2 != 0 {
            new_carry = binomial_tree_merge(heap, &mut vals[0], &mut vals[1]);
            if new_carry.is_none() {
                binomial_heap_merge_undo(&mut new_roots, i);
                binomial_tree_unref(carry.as_mut().map(|x| &mut **x));
                return 0;
            }
        } else {
            new_carry = None;
        }

        binomial_tree_unref(carry.as_mut().map(|x| &mut **x));
        carry = new_carry;
        binomial_tree_ref(&mut carry);
    }

    for i in 0..heap.roots_length as usize {
        if heap.roots[i].is_some() {
            binomial_tree_unref(heap.roots[i].as_mut().map(|x| &mut **x));
        }
    }

    heap.roots = new_roots;
    heap.roots_length = new_roots_length as u32;
    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn binomial_heap_merge<T>(heap: &mut BinomialHeap<T>, other: &mut BinomialHeap<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut new_roots: Vec<Link<BinomialTree<T>>>;
    let mut new_roots_length: u32;
    let mut vals: [Link<BinomialTree<T>>; 3] = [None; 3];
    let mut num_vals: i32;
    let mut carry: Link<BinomialTree<T>>;
    let mut new_carry: Link<BinomialTree<T>>;
    let mut max: u32;
    let mut i: u32;

    if heap.roots_length > other.roots_length {
        max = heap.roots_length + 1;
    } else {
        max = other.roots_length + 1;
    }

    new_roots = vec![None; max as usize];

    new_roots_length = 0;
    carry = None;

    cfor!(i = 0; i < max; i += 1; {
        num_vals = 0;
        if i < heap.roots_length && heap.roots[i as usize].is_some() {
            vals[num_vals as usize] = heap.roots[i as usize];
            num_vals += 1;
        }

        if i < other.roots_length && other.roots[i as usize].is_some() {
            vals[num_vals as usize] = other.roots[i as usize];
            num_vals += 1;
        }

        if carry.is_some() {
            vals[num_vals as usize] = carry;
            num_vals += 1;
        }

        if num_vals & 1 != 0 {
            new_roots[i as usize] = vals[(num_vals - 1) as usize];
            binomial_tree_ref(new_roots[i as usize]);
            new_roots_length = i + 1;
        } else {
            new_roots[i as usize] = None;
        }

        if num_vals & 2 != 0 {
            new_carry = binomial_tree_merge(heap, vals[0], vals[1]);

            if new_carry.is_none() {
                binomial_heap_merge_undo(&mut new_roots);
                binomial_tree_unref(carry);
                return 0;
            }
        }
        else {
            new_carry = None;
        }
        binomial_tree_unref(carry);
        carry = new_carry;
        binomial_tree_ref(carry);
    });
    cfor!(i = 0; i < heap.roots_length; i += 1; {
        if heap.roots[i as usize].is_some() {
            binomial_tree_unref(heap.roots[i as usize]);
        }
    });
    heap.roots = new_roots;
    heap.roots_length = new_roots_length;
    return 1;
}

函数名: binomial_heap_insert
生成代码:
pub fn binomial_heap_insert<T>(heap: &mut BinomialHeap<T>, value: BinomialHeapValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut fake_heap = BinomialHeap::new();
    let mut new_tree = Some(Box::new(BinomialTree::new()));
    if new_tree.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    new_tree.as_mut().unwrap().value = value;
    new_tree.as_mut().unwrap().order = 0;
    new_tree.as_mut().unwrap().refcount = 1;
    new_tree.as_mut().unwrap().subtrees = vec![];
    fake_heap.heap_type = heap.heap_type;
    fake_heap.compare_func = heap.compare_func;
    fake_heap.num_values = 1;
    fake_heap.roots = vec![new_tree.take()];
    fake_heap.roots_length = 1;
    let result = binomial_heap_merge(heap, &mut fake_heap);
    if result != 0 {
        heap.num_values += 1;
    }
    binomial_tree_unref(new_tree.as_mut());
    result
}
标准答案:
pub fn binomial_heap_insert<T>(heap: &mut BinomialHeap<T>, value: BinomialHeapValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut fake_heap: BinomialHeap<T>;
    let mut new_tree: Link<BinomialTree<T>>;
    let mut result: i32;

    let mut new_tree = Link::new(BinomialTree::new());
    new_tree.borrow_mut().value = value;
    new_tree.borrow_mut().order = 0;
    new_tree.borrow_mut().refcount = 1;
    new_tree.borrow_mut().subtrees = vec![];

    fake_heap = BinomialHeap::new();
    fake_heap.heap_type = heap.heap_type;
    fake_heap.compare_func = heap.compare_func;
    fake_heap.num_values = 1;
    fake_heap.roots = vec![new_tree];
    fake_heap.roots_length = 1;

    result = binomial_heap_merge(heap, &mut fake_heap);

    if result != 0 {
        heap.num_values += 1;
    }

    binomial_tree_unref(new_tree);

    return result;
}

函数名: binomial_heap_pop
生成代码:
pub fn binomial_heap_pop<T>(heap: &mut BinomialHeap<T>) -> BinomialHeapValue<T> {
    if heap.num_values == 0 {
        return BINOMIAL_HEAP_NULL;
    }

    let mut least_index = u32::MAX;
    for i in 0..heap.roots_length {
        if heap.roots[i as usize].is_none() {
            continue;
        }
        if least_index == u32::MAX || binomial_heap_cmp(heap, heap.roots[i as usize].as_ref().unwrap().value, heap.roots[least_index as usize].as_ref().unwrap().value) < 0 {
            least_index = i;
        }
    }

    let least_tree = heap.roots[least_index as usize].take();
    if least_tree.is_none() {
        return BINOMIAL_HEAP_NULL;
    }

    let mut fake_heap = BinomialHeap::new();
    fake_heap.heap_type = heap.heap_type;
    fake_heap.compare_func = heap.compare_func;
    fake_heap.roots = least_tree.as_ref().unwrap().subtrees.clone();
    fake_heap.roots_length = least_tree.as_ref().unwrap().order as u32;

    if binomial_heap_merge(heap, &mut fake_heap) != 0 {
        let result = least_tree.as_ref().unwrap().value;
        binomial_tree_unref(least_tree);
        heap.num_values -= 1;
        return result;
    } else {
        heap.roots[least_index as usize] = least_tree;
        return BINOMIAL_HEAP_NULL;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn binomial_heap_pop<T>(heap: &mut BinomialHeap<T>) -> BinomialHeapValue<T> {
    let mut least_tree: Link<BinomialTree<T>>;
    let mut fake_heap: BinomialHeap<T>;
    let mut result: BinomialHeapValue<T>;
    let mut i: u32;
    let mut least_index: u32;

    if heap.num_values == 0 {
        return binomial_heap_null!();
    }

    least_index = u32::MAX;

    cfor!(i = 0; i < heap.roots_length; i += 1; {
        if heap.roots[i as usize].is_none() {
            continue;
        }

        if least_index == u32::MAX || binomial_heap_cmp(heap, &heap.roots[i as usize].borrow().value, &heap.roots[least_index as usize].borrow().value) < 0 {
            least_index = i;
        }
    });
    least_tree = heap.roots[least_index as usize];
    heap.roots[least_index as usize] = None;

    fake_heap = BinomialHeap::new();
    fake_heap.heap_type = heap.heap_type;
    fake_heap.compare_func = heap.compare_func;
    fake_heap.roots = least_tree.borrow_mut().subtrees.clone();
    fake_heap.roots_length = least_tree.borrow().order as u32;

    if binomial_heap_merge(heap, &mut fake_heap) != 0 {
        result = least_tree.borrow_mut().value.take();
        binomial_tree_unref(least_tree);
        heap.num_values -= 1;
        return result;
    } else {
        heap.roots[least_index as usize] = least_tree;
        return binomial_heap_null!();
    }
}

函数名: bloom_filter_new
生成代码:
pub fn bloom_filter_new(table_size: u32, hash_func: BloomFilterHashFunc, num_functions: u32) -> Option<BloomFilter> {
    if num_functions > (salts.len() as u32) {
        return None;
    }
    let mut filter = BloomFilter::new();
    filter.table = vec![0; ((table_size + 7) / 8) as usize];
    if filter.table.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    filter.hash_func = hash_func;
    filter.num_functions = num_functions;
    filter.table_size = table_size;
    Some(filter)
}
标准答案:
pub fn bloom_filter_new<T>(table_size: u32, hash_func: BloomFilterHashFunc<T>, num_functions: u32) -> Option<BloomFilter<T>> {
    if num_functions > SALTS.len() as u32 {
        return None;
    }
    let mut filter = BloomFilter::new();
    filter.table = vec![0; (table_size + 7) as usize / 8];
    filter.hash_func = hash_func;
    filter.num_functions = num_functions;
    filter.table_size = table_size;
    Some(filter)
}

函数名: bloom_filter_free
生成代码:
pub fn bloom_filter_free(bloomfilter: *mut BloomFilter) {
    unsafe {
        if !bloomfilter.is_null() {
            let table = (*bloomfilter).table.as_mut_ptr();
            if !table.is_null() {
                std::mem::drop((*bloomfilter).table);
            }
            std::mem::drop(bloomfilter);
        }
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn bloom_filter_free<T>(bloomfilter: &mut BloomFilter<T>) {
}

函数名: bloom_filter_insert
生成代码:
pub fn bloom_filter_insert(bloomfilter: &mut BloomFilter, value: BloomFilterValue<Box<dyn std::any::Any>>) {
    let mut hash: u32;
    let mut subhash: u32;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut i: u32;
    let mut b: u8;
    hash = (bloomfilter.hash_func)(value);
    for i in 0..bloomfilter.num_functions {
        subhash = hash ^ salts[i as usize];
        index = subhash % bloomfilter.table_size;
        b = 1 << (index % 8);
        bloomfilter.table[(index / 8) as usize] |= b;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn bloom_filter_insert<T>(bloomfilter: &mut BloomFilter<T>, value: BloomFilterValue<T>) {
    let mut hash: u32;
    let mut subhash: u32;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut i: u32;
    let mut b: u8;

    hash = (bloomfilter.hash_func)(&value);
    cfor!(i = 0; i < bloomfilter.num_functions; i += 1; {
        subhash = hash ^ SALTS[i as usize];
        index = subhash % bloomfilter.table_size;
        b = 1 << (index % 8);
        bloomfilter.table[(index / 8) as usize] |= b;
    });
}

函数名: bloom_filter_query
生成代码:
pub fn bloom_filter_query<T>(bloomfilter: &BloomFilter, value: BloomFilterValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut hash: u32;
    let mut subhash: u32;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut i: u32;
    let mut b: u8;
    let mut bit: u8;

    hash = (bloomfilter.hash_func)(value);
    for i in 0..bloomfilter.num_functions {
        subhash = hash ^ salts[i as usize];
        index = subhash % bloomfilter.table_size;
        b = bloomfilter.table[(index / 8) as usize];
        bit = 1 << (index % 8);
        if (b & bit) == 0 {
            return 0;
        }
    }
    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn bloom_filter_query<T>(bloomfilter: &mut BloomFilter<T>, value: &mut BloomFilterValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut hash: u32;
    let mut subhash: u32;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut i: u32;
    let mut b: u8;
    let mut bit: u8;
    hash = (bloomfilter.hash_func)(value);
    cfor!(i = 0; i < bloomfilter.num_functions; i += 1; {
        subhash = hash ^ SALTS[i as usize];
        index = subhash % bloomfilter.table_size;
        b = bloomfilter.table[(index / 8) as usize];
        bit = 1 << (index % 8);

        if (b & bit) == 0 {
            return 0;
        }
    });
    return 1;
}

函数名: bloom_filter_union
生成代码:
pub fn bloom_filter_union(filter1: &BloomFilter, filter2: &BloomFilter) -> Option<BloomFilter> {
    if filter1.table_size != filter2.table_size
        || filter1.num_functions != filter2.num_functions
        || filter1.hash_func as usize != filter2.hash_func as usize {
        return None;
    }

    let result = bloom_filter_new(filter1.table_size, filter1.hash_func, filter1.num_functions)?;

    let array_size = (filter1.table_size as usize + 7) / 8;
    for i in 0..array_size {
        result.table[i] = filter1.table[i] | filter2.table[i];
    }

    Some(result)
}
标准答案:
pub fn bloom_filter_union<T>(filter1: &mut BloomFilter<T>, filter2: &mut BloomFilter<T>) -> Option<BloomFilter<T>> {
    let mut result: BloomFilter<T>;
    let mut i: u32;
    let mut array_size: u32;

    if filter1.table_size != filter2.table_size || filter1.num_functions != filter2.num_functions || filter1.hash_func != filter2.hash_func {
        return None;
    }

    result = BloomFilter::new();
    result.table = vec![0; (filter1.table_size + 7) as usize / 8];
    result.hash_func = filter1.hash_func;
    result.num_functions = filter1.num_functions;
    result.table_size = filter1.table_size;

    array_size = (filter1.table_size + 7) / 8;

    cfor!(i = 0; i < array_size; i += 1; {
        result.table[i as usize] = filter1.table[i as usize] | filter2.table[i as usize];
    });

    Some(result)
}

函数名: int_equal
生成代码:
pub fn int_equal(vlocation1: *const std::ffi::c_void, vlocation2: *const std::ffi::c_void) -> bool {
    let location1: *const i32 = vlocation1 as *const i32;
    let location2: *const i32 = vlocation2 as *const i32;
    unsafe { *location1 == *location2 }
}

函数名: string_nocase_compare
生成代码:
pub fn string_nocase_compare(string1: *const c_char, string2: *const c_char) -> i32 {
    let mut p1 = string1;
    let mut p2 = string2;
    loop {
        let c1 = unsafe { tolower(*p1) };
        let c2 = unsafe { tolower(*p2) };
        if c1 != c2 {
            if c1 < c2 {
                return -1;
            } else {
                return 1;
            }
        }
        if c1 == 0 {
            break;
        }
        p1 = unsafe { p1.offset(1) };
        p2 = unsafe { p2.offset(1) };
    }
    return 0;
}
标准答案:
pub fn string_nocase_compare(string1: &Option<String>, string2: &Option<String>) -> i32 {
	let mut p1 = string1.as_deref().unwrap_or(&"").chars().peekable();
	let mut p2 = string2.as_deref().unwrap_or(&"").chars().peekable();
	loop {
		let c1 = p1.peek().unwrap_or(&'\0').to_ascii_lowercase();
		let c2 = p2.peek().unwrap_or(&'\0').to_ascii_lowercase();
		if c1 != c2 {
			if c1 < c2 {
				return -1;
			} else {
				return 1;
			}
		}
		if c1 == '\0' {
			break;
		}
		p1.next();
		p2.next();
	}
	return 0;
}

函数名: string_hash
生成代码:
pub fn string_hash(string: *const c_void) -> u32 {
    let mut result: u32 = 5381;
    let mut p = string as *const u8;
    unsafe {
        while *p != b'\0' {
            result = (result << 5).wrapping_add(result).wrapping_add(*p as u32);
            p = p.offset(1);
        }
    }
    result
}
标准答案:
pub fn string_hash(string: &Option<String>) -> u32 {
	let mut result: u32 = 5381;
	let mut p = string.as_deref().unwrap_or(&"").chars().peekable();

	while p.peek().unwrap_or(&'\0') != &'\0' {
		result = (result << 5) + result + *p.peek().unwrap_or(&'\0') as u32;
		p.next();
	}

	return result;
}

pub fn string_nocase_hash(string: &str) -> u32 {
    let mut result: u32 = 5381;
    for c in string.chars() {
        if c != '\0' {
            result = (result << 5).wrapping_add(result).wrapping_add(c.to_ascii_lowercase() as u32);
        }
    }
    result
}

pub fn string_nocase_hash(string: &Option<String>) -> u32 {
	let mut result: u32 = 5381;
	let mut p = string.as_deref().unwrap_or(&"").chars().peekable();

	while p.peek().unwrap_or(&'\0') != &'\0' {
		result = (result << 5) + result + p.peek().unwrap_or(&'\0').to_ascii_lowercase() as u32;
		p.next();
	}

	return result;

函数名: hash_table_allocate_table
生成代码:
pub fn hash_table_allocate_table<K, V>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>) -> bool {
    let new_table_size: u32;
    if hash_table.prime_index < HASH_TABLE_NUM_PRIMES {
        new_table_size = HASH_TABLE_PRIMES[hash_table.prime_index as usize];
    } else {
        new_table_size = hash_table.entries * 10;
    }
    hash_table.table_size = new_table_size;
    hash_table.table = vec![None; new_table_size as usize];
    hash_table.table.iter().all(|entry| entry.is_none())
}
标准答案:
fn hash_table_allocate_table<K, V>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>) -> bool {
    let new_table_size: u32;
    if hash_table.prime_index < HASH_TABLE_NUM_PRIMES {
        new_table_size = HASH_TABLE_PRIMES[hash_table.prime_index as usize];
    } else {
        new_table_size = hash_table.entries * 10;
    }

    hash_table.table_size = new_table_size;
    hash_table.table = vec![None; hash_table.table_size as usize];
    true
}

函数名: hash_table_free_entry
生成代码:
pub fn hash_table_free_entry<K, V>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>, entry: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>) {
    if let Some(entry_ref) = entry.as_ref() {
        let pair = &entry_ref.pair;
        if let Some(key_free_func) = hash_table.key_free_func {
            if let Some(key) = pair.key.as_ref() {
                key_free_func(Some(key));
            }
        }
        if let Some(value_free_func) = hash_table.value_free_func {
            if let Some(value) = pair.value.as_ref() {
                value_free_func(Some(value));
            }
        }
    }
    drop(entry);
}
标准答案:
fn hash_table_free_entry<K, V>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>, mut entry: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>) {
    let mut pair = &mut entry.borrow_mut().pair;
    (hash_table.key_free_func)(&mut pair.key);
    (hash_table.value_free_func)(&mut pair.value);
    entry.drop();
}

函数名: hash_table_new
生成代码:
pub fn hash_table_new<K, V>(hash_func: fn(HashTableKey<K>) -> u32, equal_func: fn(HashTableKey<K>, HashTableKey<K>) -> bool) -> Option<Link<HashTable<K, V>>> {
    let hash_table = Box::new(HashTable::new());
    if hash_table.is_none() {
        return None;
    }
    hash_table.borrow_mut().hash_func = Some(hash_func);
    hash_table.borrow_mut().equal_func = Some(equal_func);
    hash_table.borrow_mut().key_free_func = None;
    hash_table.borrow_mut().value_free_func = None;
    hash_table.borrow_mut().entries = 0;
    hash_table.borrow_mut().prime_index = 0;
    if !hash_table_allocate_table(&mut hash_table.borrow_mut()) {
        return None;
    }
    Some(hash_table)
}
标准答案:
pub fn hash_table_new<K, V>(hash_func: HashTableHashFunc<K>, equal_func: HashTableEqualFunc<K>) -> Option<HashTable<K, V>> {
    let mut hash_table: HashTable<K, V>;
    hash_table = HashTable::new();
    hash_table.hash_func = hash_func;
    hash_table.equal_func = equal_func;
    hash_table.key_free_func = |value| {};
    hash_table.value_free_func = |value| {};
    hash_table.entries = 0;
    hash_table.prime_index = 0;

    if !hash_table_allocate_table(&mut hash_table) {
        return None;
    }

    Some(hash_table)
}

函数名: hash_table_free
生成代码:
pub fn hash_table_free<K, V>(hash_table: &mut Link<HashTable<K, V>>) {
    if let Some(hash_table) = hash_table {
        let mut rover: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
        let mut next: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
        for i in 0..hash_table.table_size {
            rover = hash_table.table[i as usize].take();
            while rover.is_some() {
                next = rover.as_ref().unwrap().next.take();
                hash_table_free_entry(hash_table, rover);
                rover = next;
            }
        }
        hash_table.table.clear();
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn hash_table_free<K, V>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>) {
    let mut rover: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut next: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut i: u32;
    cfor!(i = 0; i < hash_table.table_size; i += 1; {
        rover = hash_table.table[i as usize];
        while rover.is_some() {
            next = rover.borrow().next;
            hash_table_free_entry(hash_table, rover);
            rover = next;
        }
    });
}

函数名: hash_table_enlarge
生成代码:
pub fn hash_table_enlarge<K, V>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>) -> bool {
    let old_table: Vec<Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>>;
    let old_table_size: u32;
    let old_prime_index: u32;
    let mut rover: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut next: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut i: u32;

    old_table = std::mem::take(&mut hash_table.table);
    old_table_size = hash_table.table_size;
    old_prime_index = hash_table.prime_index;
    hash_table.prime_index += 1;

    if !hash_table_allocate_table(hash_table) {
        hash_table.table = old_table;
        hash_table.table_size = old_table_size;
        hash_table.prime_index = old_prime_index;
        return false;
    }

    for i in 0..old_table_size {
        rover = old_table[i as usize].take();
        while rover.is_some() {
            next = rover.as_ref().unwrap().next.take();
            let pair = &rover.as_ref().unwrap().pair;
            index = (hash_table.hash_func.unwrap())(pair.key.clone()) % hash_table.table_size;
            rover.as_mut().unwrap().next = hash_table.table[index as usize].take();
            hash_table.table[index as usize] = rover;
            rover = next;
        }
    }

    drop(old_table);
    true
}
标准答案:
pub fn hash_table_enlarge<K, V>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>) -> i32 {
    let mut old_table: Vec<Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>>;
    let mut old_table_size: u32;
    let mut old_prime_index: u32;
    let mut rover: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut pair: &mut HashTablePair<K, V>;
    let mut next: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut i: u32;
    old_table = std::mem::take(&mut hash_table.table);
    old_table_size = hash_table.table_size;
    old_prime_index = hash_table.prime_index;
    hash_table.prime_index += 1;

    if !hash_table_allocate_table(hash_table) {
        hash_table.table = old_table;
        hash_table.table_size = old_table_size;
        hash_table.prime_index = old_prime_index;
        return 0;
    }
    cfor!(i = 0; i < old_table_size; i += 1; {
        rover = old_table[i as usize];
        while rover.is_some() {
            next = rover.borrow().next;
            pair = &mut rover.borrow_mut().pair;
            index = (hash_table.hash_func)(&pair.key) % hash_table.table_size;
            rover.borrow_mut().next = hash_table.table[index as usize];
            hash_table.table[index as usize] = rover;
            rover = next;
        }
    });
    1
}

函数名: hash_table_insert
生成代码:
pub fn hash_table_insert<K, V>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>, key: HashTableKey<K>, value: HashTableValue<V>) -> i32 {
    let mut rover: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut pair: &mut HashTablePair<K, V>;
    let mut newentry: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut index: u32;

    if (hash_table.entries * 3) / hash_table.table_size > 0 {
        if !hash_table_enlarge(hash_table) {
            return 0;
        }
    }

    index = (hash_table.hash_func.unwrap())(key) % hash_table.table_size;
    rover = hash_table.table[index as usize].clone();

    while rover.is_some() {
        pair = &mut rover.as_mut().unwrap().pair;
        if (hash_table.equal_func.unwrap())(pair.key.clone(), key.clone()) {
            if hash_table.value_free_func.is_some() {
                (hash_table.value_free_func.unwrap())(pair.value.take());
            }
            if hash_table.key_free_func.is_some() {
                (hash_table.key_free_func.unwrap())(pair.key.take());
            }
            pair.key = key.clone();
            pair.value = value.clone();
            return 1;
        }
        rover = rover.unwrap().next.clone();
    }

    newentry = Some(Box::new(HashTableEntry::new()));
    if newentry.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }

    newentry.as_mut().unwrap().pair.key = key;
    newentry.as_mut().unwrap().pair.value = value;
    newentry.as_mut().unwrap().next = hash_table.table[index as usize].clone();
    hash_table.table[index as usize] = newentry;
    hash_table.entries += 1;
    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn hash_table_insert<K, V>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>, key: HashTableKey<K>, value: HashTableValue<V>) -> i32 {
    let mut rover: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut pair: &mut HashTablePair<K, V>;
    let mut newentry: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut index: u32;
    if (hash_table.entries * 3) / hash_table.table_size > 0 {
        if hash_table_enlarge(hash_table) == 0 {
            return 0;
        }
    }
    index = (hash_table.hash_func)(&key) % hash_table.table_size;
    rover = hash_table.table[index as usize];
    while rover.is_some() {
        pair = &mut rover.borrow_mut().pair;
        if (hash_table.equal_func)(&pair.key, &key) != 0 {
            (hash_table.value_free_func)(&mut pair.value);
            (hash_table.key_free_func)(&mut pair.key);
            pair.key = key;
            pair.value = value;
            return 1;
        }
        rover = rover.borrow().next;
    }
    
    let mut newentry = HashTableEntry::new();
    newentry.pair.key = key;
    newentry.pair.value = value;
    newentry.next = hash_table.table[index as usize];

    let mut newentry = Link::new(newentry);

    hash_table.table[index as usize] = newentry;
    hash_table.entries += 1;
    1
}

函数名: hash_table_lookup
生成代码:
pub fn hash_table_lookup<K, V>(hash_table: &HashTable<K, V>, key: HashTableKey<K>) -> HashTableValue<V> {
    let mut rover: &Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut pair: &HashTablePair<K, V>;
    let index: u32;

    if let Some(hash_func) = hash_table.hash_func {
        index = hash_func(key) % hash_table.table_size;
    } else {
        return HASH_TABLE_NULL;
    }

    rover = &hash_table.table[index as usize];

    while let Some(entry) = rover {
        pair = &entry.pair;
        if let Some(equal_func) = hash_table.equal_func {
            if equal_func(key.clone(), pair.key.clone()) {
                return pair.value.clone();
            }
        }
        rover = &entry.next;
    }

    HASH_TABLE_NULL
}
标准答案:
pub fn hash_table_lookup<K, V: Clone>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>, key: HashTableKey<K>) -> HashTableValue<V> {
    let mut rover: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut pair: &mut HashTablePair<K, V>;
    let mut index: u32;
    index = (hash_table.hash_func)(&key) % hash_table.table_size;
    rover = hash_table.table[index as usize];
    while rover.is_some() {
        pair = &mut rover.borrow_mut().pair;
        if (hash_table.equal_func)(&pair.key, &key) != 0 {
            return pair.value.clone();
        }
        rover = rover.borrow().next;
    }
    hash_table_null!()
}

函数名: hash_table_remove
生成代码:
pub fn hash_table_remove<K, V>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>, key: HashTableKey<K>) -> i32 {
    let mut rover: &mut Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut entry: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut pair: &HashTablePair<K, V>;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut result: i32;

    index = (hash_table.hash_func.unwrap())(key) % hash_table.table_size;
    result = 0;
    rover = &mut hash_table.table[index as usize];

    while rover.is_some() {
        pair = &rover.as_ref().unwrap().pair;
        if (hash_table.equal_func.unwrap())(key, pair.key) {
            entry = rover.take().unwrap();
            *rover = entry.next.take();
            hash_table_free_entry(hash_table, entry);
            hash_table.entries -= 1;
            result = 1;
            break;
        }
        rover = &mut rover.as_mut().unwrap().next;
    }

    result
}
标准答案:
pub fn hash_table_remove<K, V>(hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>, key: HashTableKey<K>) -> i32 {
    let mut rover: LinkRover<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut entry: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut pair: &mut HashTablePair<K, V>;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut result: i32;
    index = (hash_table.hash_func)(&key) % hash_table.table_size;
    result = 0;
    rover = hash_table.table[index as usize].rover();
    while rover.is_some() {
        pair = &mut rover.borrow_mut().pair;
        if (hash_table.equal_func)(&pair.key, &key) != 0 {
            entry = *rover;
            *rover = entry.borrow().next;
            hash_table_free_entry(hash_table, entry);
            hash_table.entries -= 1;
            result = 1;
            break;
        }
        rover = rover.borrow_mut().next.rover();
    }
    result
}

pub fn hash_table_iterate<K, V>(hash_table: Link<HashTable<K, V>>, iterator: &mut HashTableIterator<K, V>) {
    let mut chain: u32 = 0;
    iterator.hash_table = hash_table.clone();
    iterator.next_entry = None;
    for chain in 0..hash_table.borrow().table_size {
        if hash_table.borrow().table[chain as usize].is_some() {
            iterator.next_entry = hash_table.borrow().table[chain as usize].clone();
            iterator.next_chain = chain;
            break;
        }
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn hash_table_iterate<'a, K, V>(hash_table: &'a mut HashTable<K, V>, iterator: &mut HashTableIterator<'a, K, V>) {
    let mut chain: u32;
    iterator.hash_table = hash_table;
    let mut hash_table = &mut iterator.hash_table;
    iterator.next_entry = None;
    cfor!(chain = 0; chain < hash_table.table_size; chain += 1; {
        if hash_table.table[chain as usize].is_some() {
            iterator.next_entry = hash_table.table[chain as usize];
            iterator.next_chain = chain;
            break;
        }
    });
}

函数名: hash_table_iter_next
生成代码:
pub fn hash_table_iter_next<K, V>(iterator: &mut HashTableIterator<K, V>) -> HashTablePair<K, V> {
    let mut pair = HashTablePair { key: None, value: None };
    let hash_table = iterator.hash_table.as_ref().unwrap();

    if iterator.next_entry.is_none() {
        return pair;
    }

    let current_entry = iterator.next_entry.take().unwrap();
    pair = current_entry.pair;

    if current_entry.next.is_some() {
        iterator.next_entry = current_entry.next;
    } else {
        let mut chain = iterator.next_chain + 1;
        iterator.next_entry = None;

        while chain < hash_table.table_size {
            if hash_table.table[chain as usize].is_some() {
                iterator.next_entry = hash_table.table[chain as usize];
                break;
            }
            chain += 1;
        }

        iterator.next_chain = chain;
    }

    pair
}
标准答案:
pub fn hash_table_iter_next<K: Clone, V: Clone>(iterator: &mut HashTableIterator<K, V>) -> HashTablePair<K, V> {
    let mut current_entry: Link<HashTableEntry<K, V>>;
    let mut hash_table: &mut HashTable<K, V>;
    let mut pair: HashTablePair<K, V> = HashTablePair::new();
    let mut chain: u32;
    hash_table = iterator.hash_table;
    if iterator.next_entry.is_none() {
        return pair;
    }
    current_entry = iterator.next_entry;
    pair = current_entry.borrow_mut().pair.clone();
    if current_entry.borrow().next.is_some() {
        iterator.next_entry = current_entry.borrow().next;
    } else {
        chain = iterator.next_chain + 1;
        iterator.next_entry = None;

        while chain < hash_table.table_size {
            if hash_table.table[chain as usize].is_some() {
                iterator.next_entry = hash_table.table[chain as usize];
                break;
            }
            chain += 1;
        }
        iterator.next_chain = chain;
    }
    pair
}

pub fn list_free<T>(list: Link<ListEntry<T>>) {
    let mut entry = list;
    while entry.is_some() {
        let next = entry.as_ref().unwrap().next.take();
        drop(entry);
        entry = next;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn list_free<T>(list: Link<ListEntry<T>>) {
    let mut entry = list;
    while entry.is_some() {
        let next = entry.borrow().next;
        entry.drop();
        entry = next;
    }
}

函数名: list_prepend
生成代码:
pub fn list_prepend<T>(list: &mut Link<ListEntry<T>>, data: ListValue<T>) -> Link<ListEntry<T>> {
    if list.is_none() {
        return None;
    }
    let newentry = Link::new(ListEntry::new());
    if newentry.is_none() {
        return None;
    }
    newentry.as_ref().unwrap().borrow_mut().data = data;
    if list.as_ref().unwrap().borrow().next.is_some() {
        list.as_ref().unwrap().borrow_mut().prev = newentry.clone();
    }
    newentry.as_ref().unwrap().borrow_mut().prev = None;
    newentry.as_ref().unwrap().borrow_mut().next = list.clone();
    *list = newentry.clone();
    newentry
}
标准答案:
pub fn list_prepend<T>(list: &mut Link<ListEntry<T>>, data: ListValue<T>) -> Link<ListEntry<T>> {
    let mut newentry = ListEntry::new();
    newentry.data = data;
    newentry.prev = None;
    newentry.next = *list;
    let mut newentry = Link::new(newentry);
    if list.is_some() {
        list.borrow_mut().prev = newentry;
    }
    *list = newentry;
    newentry
}

函数名: list_append
生成代码:
pub fn list_append<T>(list: &mut Link<ListEntry<T>>, data: ListValue<T>) -> Link<ListEntry<T>> {
    if list.is_none() {
        return None;
    }

    let newentry = Some(Box::new(ListEntry {
        data,
        prev: None,
        next: None,
    }));

    if newentry.is_none() {
        return None;
    }

    let newentry = newentry.unwrap();

    if list.is_none() {
        *list = Some(newentry);
        list.as_mut().unwrap().prev = None;
    } else {
        let mut rover = list.as_mut().unwrap();
        while rover.next.is_some() {
            rover = rover.next.as_mut().unwrap();
        }
        newentry.prev = Some(rover.clone());
        rover.next = Some(newentry);
    }

    newentry
}
标准答案:
pub fn list_append<T>(list: &mut Link<ListEntry<T>>, data: ListValue<T>) -> Link<ListEntry<T>> {
    let mut rover: Link<ListEntry<T>>;
    let mut newentry: Link<ListEntry<T>>;
    let mut newentry = ListEntry::new();
    newentry.data = data;
    newentry.next = None;
    let mut newentry = Link::new(newentry);
    if list.is_none() {
        *list = newentry;
        newentry.borrow_mut().prev = None;
    } else {
        cfor!(rover = *list; rover.borrow().next.is_some(); rover = rover.borrow().next; {});
        newentry.borrow_mut().prev = rover;
        rover.borrow_mut().next = newentry;
    }
    newentry
}

函数名: list_to_array
生成代码:
pub fn list_to_array<T>(list: Link<ListEntry<T>>) -> Option<Vec<ListValue<T>>> {
    let mut rover = list;
    let length = list_length(rover);
    let mut array = Vec::with_capacity(length);

    for _ in 0..length {
        if let Some(entry) = rover {
            array.push(entry.borrow().data);
            rover = entry.borrow().next.clone();
        }
    }

    Some(array)
}
标准答案:
pub fn list_to_array<T: Clone>(list: Link<ListEntry<T>>) -> Vec<ListValue<T>> {
    let mut rover: Link<ListEntry<T>>;
    let mut array: Vec<ListValue<T>>;
    let mut length: u32;
    let mut i: u32;

    let length = list_length(list);
    let mut array = vec![None; length as usize];
    let mut rover = list;
    cfor!(i = 0; i < length; i += 1; {
        array[i as usize] = rover.borrow().data.clone();
        rover = rover.borrow().next;
    });
    array
}

函数名: list_remove_entry
生成代码:
pub fn list_remove_entry<T>(list: &mut Link<ListEntry<T>>, entry: Link<ListEntry<T>>) -> i32 {
    if list.is_none() || entry.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }

    let entry_ref = entry.as_ref().unwrap();
    if entry_ref.prev.is_none() {
        *list = entry_ref.next.clone();
        if entry_ref.next.is_some() {
            entry_ref.next.as_ref().unwrap().borrow_mut().prev = None;
        }
    } else {
        entry_ref.prev.as_ref().unwrap().borrow_mut().next = entry_ref.next.clone();
        if entry_ref.next.is_some() {
            entry_ref.next.as_ref().unwrap().borrow_mut().prev = entry_ref.prev.clone();
        }
    }

    drop(entry);
    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn list_remove_entry<T>(list: &mut Link<ListEntry<T>>, mut entry: Link<ListEntry<T>>) -> i32 {
    if list.is_none() || entry.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    if entry.borrow().prev.is_none() {
        *list = entry.borrow().next;
        if entry.borrow().next.is_some() {
            entry.borrow_mut().next.borrow_mut().prev = None;
        }
    } else {
        entry.borrow_mut().prev.borrow_mut().next = entry.borrow().next;
        if entry.borrow().next.is_some() {
            entry.borrow_mut().next.borrow_mut().prev = entry.borrow().prev;
        }
    }
    entry.drop();
    return 1;
}

函数名: list_remove_data
生成代码:
pub fn list_remove_data<T>(list: &mut Link<ListEntry<T>>, callback: ListEqualFunc<T>, data: ListValue<T>) -> u32 {
    let mut entries_removed = 0;
    let mut rover = list.take();

    while let Some(mut entry) = rover {
        rover = entry.next.take();
        if callback(entry.data, data) != 0 {
            if entry.prev.is_none() {
                *list = entry.next.take();
            } else {
                if let Some(prev) = entry.prev.as_mut() {
                    prev.next = entry.next.take();
                }
            }
            if let Some(next) = entry.next.as_mut() {
                next.prev = entry.prev.take();
            }
            entries_removed += 1;
        } else {
            entry.next = rover;
            rover = Some(entry);
        }
    }

    entries_removed
}
标准答案:
pub fn list_remove_data<T>(list: &mut Link<ListEntry<T>>, callback: ListEqualFunc<T>, data: &mut ListValue<T>) -> u32 {
    let mut entries_removed: u32;
    let mut rover: Link<ListEntry<T>>;
    let mut next: Link<ListEntry<T>>;
    if list.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    entries_removed = 0;
    rover = *list;
    while rover.is_some() {
        next = rover.borrow().next;
        if callback(&rover.borrow().data, &data) != 0 {
            if rover.borrow().prev.is_none() {
                *list = rover.borrow().next;
            } else {
                rover.borrow_mut().prev.borrow_mut().next = rover.borrow().next;
            }
            if rover.borrow().next.is_some() {
                rover.borrow_mut().next.borrow_mut().prev = rover.borrow().prev;
            }
            rover.drop();
            entries_removed += 1;
        }
        rover = next;
    }
    entries_removed
}

函数名: list_sort_internal
生成代码:
pub fn list_sort_internal<T>(list: &mut Link<ListEntry<T>>, compare_func: ListCompareFunc<T>) -> Link<ListEntry<T>> {
    if list.is_none() || compare_func.is_none() {
        return None;
    }
    if list.as_ref().unwrap().next.is_none() {
        return list.clone();
    }

    let pivot = list.take().unwrap();
    let mut less_list: Link<ListEntry<T>> = None;
    let mut more_list: Link<ListEntry<T>> = None;
    let mut rover = pivot.next.take();

    while let Some(mut entry) = rover {
        rover = entry.next.take();
        if compare_func(entry.data.clone(), pivot.data.clone()) < 0 {
            entry.prev = None;
            entry.next = less_list.take();
            if let Some(less) = entry.next.as_mut() {
                less.prev = Some(entry.clone());
            }
            less_list = Some(entry);
        } else {
            entry.prev = None;
            entry.next = more_list.take();
            if let Some(more) = entry.next.as_mut() {
                more.prev = Some(entry.clone());
            }
            more_list = Some(entry);
        }
    }

    let less_list_end = list_sort_internal(&mut less_list, compare_func);
    let more_list_end = list_sort_internal(&mut more_list, compare_func);

    *list = less_list;
    if less_list.is_none() {
        pivot.prev = None;
        *list = Some(pivot.clone());
    } else {
        pivot.prev = less_list_end.clone();
        if let Some(less_end) = less_list_end {
            less_end.next = Some(pivot.clone());
        }
    }

    pivot.next = more_list;
    if let Some(more) = more_list {
        more.prev = Some(pivot.clone());
    }

    if more_list.is_none() {
        Some(pivot)
    } else {
        more_list_end
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn list_sort_internal<T>(list: &mut Link<ListEntry<T>>, compare_func: ListCompareFunc<T>) -> Link<ListEntry<T>> {
    let mut pivot: Link<ListEntry<T>>;
    let mut rover: Link<ListEntry<T>>;
    let mut less_list: Link<ListEntry<T>>;
    let mut more_list: Link<ListEntry<T>>;
    let mut less_list_end: Link<ListEntry<T>>;
    let mut more_list_end: Link<ListEntry<T>>;

    if list.is_none() {
        return None;
    }

    if list.borrow().next.is_none() {
        return *list;
    }

    pivot = *list;

    less_list = None;
    more_list = None;
    rover = list.borrow().next;

    while rover.is_some() {
        let next = rover.borrow().next;
        if compare_func(&rover.borrow().data, &pivot.borrow().data) < 0 {
            rover.borrow_mut().prev = None;
            rover.borrow_mut().next = less_list;
            if less_list.is_some() {
                less_list.borrow_mut().prev = rover;
            }
            less_list = rover;
        } else {
            rover.borrow_mut().prev = None;
            rover.borrow_mut().next = more_list;
            if more_list.is_some() {
                more_list.borrow_mut().prev = rover;
            }
            more_list = rover;
        }
        rover = next;
    }

    less_list_end = list_sort_internal(&mut less_list, compare_func);
    more_list_end = list_sort_internal(&mut more_list, compare_func);

    *list = less_list;

    if less_list.is_none() {
        pivot.borrow_mut().prev = None;
        *list = pivot;
    } else {
        pivot.borrow_mut().prev = less_list_end;
        less_list_end.borrow_mut().next = pivot;
    }

    pivot.borrow_mut().next = more_list;
    if more_list.is_some() {
        more_list.borrow_mut().prev = pivot;
    }

    if more_list.is_none() {
        return pivot;
    } else {
        return more_list_end;
    }
}

        return (*iter.prev_next).is_some();
    } else {
        return iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().next.is_some();
    }
}

pub fn list_iter_next<T>(iter: &mut ListIterator<T>) -> ListValue<T> {
    if iter.current.is_none() || iter.current != *iter.prev_next {
        iter.current = *iter.prev_next;
    } else {
        iter.prev_next = &iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().next;
        iter.current = iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().next;
    }
    if iter.current.is_none() {
        return LIST_NULL;
    } else {
        return iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().data;
    }
}

pub fn list_iter_next<T: Clone>(iter: &mut ListIterator<T>) -> ListValue<T> {

        iter.current = *iter.prev_next;
    } else {
        iter.prev_next = iter.prev_next.borrow_mut().next.rover();
        iter.current = iter.current.borrow().next;
    }
    if iter.current.is_none() {
        return list_null!();
    } else {
        return iter.current.borrow().data.clone();

pub fn list_iter_remove<T>(iter: &mut ListIterator<T>) {
    if iter.current.is_none() || iter.current != *iter.prev_next {
        return;
    } else {
        *iter.prev_next = iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().next.clone();
        if iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().next.is_some() {
            iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().next.as_ref().unwrap().prev = iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().prev.clone();
        }
        drop(iter.current.take().unwrap());
        iter.current = None;
    }
}
