函数名: queue_pop_head
生成代码:
pub fn queue_pop_head<T>(queue: &mut Queue<T>) -> QueueValue<T> {
    if queue_is_empty(queue) {
        return QUEUE_NULL;
    }
    let entry = queue.head.take().unwrap();
    queue.head = entry.next.take();
    let result = entry.data;
    if queue.head.is_none() {
        queue.tail = None;
    } else {
        queue.head.as_mut().unwrap().prev = None;
    }
    std::mem::drop(entry);
    result
}
标准答案:
pub fn queue_pop_head<T>(queue: &mut Queue<T>) -> QueueValue<T> {
    let mut entry: Link<QueueEntry<T>>;
    let result: QueueValue<T>;
    if queue_is_empty(queue) != 0 {
        return queue_null!();
    }
    entry = queue.head;
    queue.head = entry.borrow().next;
    result = entry.borrow_mut().data.take();
    if queue.head.is_none() {
        queue.tail = None;
    } else {
        queue.head.borrow_mut().prev = None;
    }
    entry.drop();
    result
}

函数名: queue_push_head

pub fn queue_push_head<T>(queue: &mut Queue<T>, data: QueueValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let new_entry = Box::new(QueueEntry::new());
    if new_entry.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    new_entry.data = data;
    new_entry.prev = None;
    new_entry.next = queue.head.take();
    if queue.head.is_none() {
        queue.head = Some(new_entry);
        queue.tail = queue.head.clone();
    } else {
        queue.head.as_mut().unwrap().prev = Some(new_entry.clone());
        queue.head = Some(new_entry);
    }
    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn queue_push_head<T>(queue: &mut Queue<T>, data: QueueValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut new_entry: Link<QueueEntry<T>>;
    let mut new_entry = QueueEntry::new();
    new_entry.data = data;
    new_entry.prev = None;
    new_entry.next = queue.head;
    let mut new_entry = Link::new(new_entry);

    if queue.head.is_none() {
        queue.head = new_entry;
        queue.tail = new_entry;
    } else {
        queue.head.borrow_mut().prev = new_entry;
        queue.head = new_entry;
    }
    1
}

pub fn queue_push_tail<T>(queue: &mut Queue<T>, data: QueueValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let new_entry = Box::new(QueueEntry::new());
    if new_entry.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    let new_entry = new_entry.unwrap();
    new_entry.data = data;
    new_entry.prev = queue.tail.take();
    new_entry.next = None;
    if queue.tail.is_none() {
        queue.head = Some(new_entry);
        queue.tail = Some(new_entry);
    } else {
        queue.tail.as_mut().unwrap().next = Some(new_entry);
        queue.tail = Some(new_entry);
    }
    return 1;
}

函数名: queue_pop_tail
生成代码:
pub fn queue_pop_tail<T>(queue: &mut Queue<T>) -> QueueValue<T> {
    if queue_is_empty(queue) {
        return QUEUE_NULL;
    }
    let entry = queue.tail.take().unwrap();
    queue.tail = entry.prev.take();
    let result = entry.data;
    if queue.tail.is_none() {
        queue.head = None;
    } else {
        queue.tail.as_mut().unwrap().next = None;
    }
    result
}
标准答案:
pub fn queue_pop_tail<T>(queue: &mut Queue<T>) -> QueueValue<T> {
    let mut entry: Link<QueueEntry<T>>;
    let result: QueueValue<T>;
    if queue_is_empty(queue) != 0 {
        return queue_null!();
    }
    entry = queue.tail;
    queue.tail = entry.borrow().prev;
    result = entry.borrow_mut().data.take();
    if queue.tail.is_none() {
        queue.head = None;
    } else {
        queue.tail.borrow_mut().next = None;
    }
    entry.drop();
    result
}

pub fn rb_tree_node_side<K, V>(node: &RBTreeNode<K, V>) -> RBTreeNodeSide {
    if node.parent.as_ref().unwrap().children[RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize].as_ref() == Some(node) {
        return RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_LEFT;
    } else {
        return RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_RIGHT;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn rb_tree_node_side<K, V>(node: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>) -> RBTreeNodeSide {
    if node.borrow().parent.borrow().children[rb_tree_node_left!()] == node {
        return rb_tree_node_left!();
    } else {
        return rb_tree_node_right!();
    }
}

pub fn rb_tree_node_sibling<K, V>(node: &RBTreeNode<K, V>) -> Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let side = rb_tree_node_side(node);
    match side {
        RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_LEFT => node.parent.as_ref().unwrap().children[1].clone(),
        RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_RIGHT => node.parent.as_ref().unwrap().children[0].clone(),
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn rb_tree_node_sibling<K, V>(node: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>) -> Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let side = rb_tree_node_side(node);
    return node.borrow().parent.borrow().children[1 - side as usize];
}

函数名: rb_tree_node_replace
生成代码:
pub fn rb_tree_node_replace<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, node1: &mut RBTreeNode<K, V>, node2: Option<&mut RBTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    let side: RBTreeNodeSide;
    if let Some(node2) = node2 {
        node2.parent = node1.parent.take();
    }
    if node1.parent.is_none() {
        tree.root_node = node2.map(|n| n as *mut RBTreeNode<K, V>).into();
    } else {
        side = rb_tree_node_side(node1);
        if let Some(parent) = node1.parent.as_mut() {
            parent.children[side as usize] = node2.map(|n| n as *mut RBTreeNode<K, V>).into();
        }
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn rb_tree_node_replace<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, mut node1: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>, mut node2: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    let side: RBTreeNodeSide;

    if node2.is_some() {
        node2.borrow_mut().parent = node1.borrow().parent;
    }

    if node1.borrow().parent.is_none() {
        tree.root_node = node2;
    } else {
        side = rb_tree_node_side(node1);
        node1.borrow_mut().parent.borrow_mut().children[side as usize] = node2;
    }
}

函数名: rb_tree_rotate
生成代码:
pub fn rb_tree_rotate<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, node: &mut RBTreeNode<K, V>, direction: RBTreeNodeSide) -> Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let new_root = node.children[1 - direction as usize].take();
    rb_tree_node_replace(tree, node, new_root.as_mut());
    node.children[1 - direction as usize] = new_root.as_ref().unwrap().children[direction as usize].take();
    new_root.as_mut().unwrap().children[direction as usize] = Some(node);
    node.parent = new_root.clone();
    if node.children[1 - direction as usize].is_some() {
        node.children[1 - direction as usize].as_mut().unwrap().parent = Some(node);
    }
    new_root
}
标准答案:
pub fn rb_tree_rotate<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, mut node: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>, direction: RBTreeNodeSide) -> Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut new_root: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>;
    new_root = node.borrow().children[1 - direction as usize];
    rb_tree_node_replace(tree, node, new_root);
    node.borrow_mut().children[1 - direction as usize] = new_root.borrow().children[direction as usize];
    new_root.borrow_mut().children[direction as usize] = node;
    node.borrow_mut().parent = new_root;
    if node.borrow().children[1 - direction as usize].is_some() {
        node.borrow_mut().children[1 - direction as usize].borrow_mut().parent = node;
    }
    return new_root;
}

    new_tree.root_node = None;
    new_tree.num_nodes = 0;

pub fn rb_tree_free_subtree<K, V>(node: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    if let Some(node) = node {
        rb_tree_free_subtree(node.borrow().children[RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize].clone());
        rb_tree_free_subtree(node.borrow().children[RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize].clone());
        drop(node);
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn rb_tree_free_subtree<K, V>(mut node: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    if node.is_some() {
        node.borrow_mut().parent = None;
        rb_tree_free_subtree(node.borrow().children[rb_tree_node_left!()]);
        rb_tree_free_subtree(node.borrow().children[rb_tree_node_right!()]);
        node.drop();
    }
}

函数名: rb_tree_insert_case3
生成代码:
pub fn rb_tree_insert_case3<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, node: &mut RBTreeNode<K, V>) {
    let grandparent: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let uncle: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>;
    grandparent = node.parent.as_ref().unwrap().parent.clone();
    uncle = rb_tree_node_uncle(node);
    if uncle.is_some() && uncle.as_ref().unwrap().color == RBTreeNodeColor::RB_TREE_NODE_RED {
        node.parent.as_mut().unwrap().color = RBTreeNodeColor::RB_TREE_NODE_BLACK;
        uncle.as_mut().unwrap().color = RBTreeNodeColor::RB_TREE_NODE_BLACK;
        grandparent.as_mut().unwrap().color = RBTreeNodeColor::RB_TREE_NODE_RED;
        rb_tree_insert_case1(tree, grandparent.as_mut().unwrap());
    } else {
        rb_tree_insert_case4(tree, node);
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn rb_tree_insert_case3<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, mut node: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    let mut grandparent: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut uncle: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>;

    grandparent = node.borrow().parent.borrow().parent;
    uncle = rb_tree_node_uncle(node);

    if uncle.is_some() && uncle.borrow().color == rb_tree_node_red!() {
        node.borrow_mut().parent.borrow_mut().color = rb_tree_node_black!();
        uncle.borrow_mut().color = rb_tree_node_black!();
        grandparent.borrow_mut().color = rb_tree_node_red!();
        rb_tree_insert_case1(tree, grandparent);
    } else {
        rb_tree_insert_case4(tree, node);
    }
}

函数名: rb_tree_insert_case4
生成代码:
pub fn rb_tree_insert_case4<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, node: &mut RBTreeNode<K, V>) {
    let mut next_node: &mut RBTreeNode<K, V>;
    let side: RBTreeNodeSide;
    side = rb_tree_node_side(node);
    if side != rb_tree_node_side(node.parent.as_ref().unwrap()) {
        next_node = node.parent.as_mut().unwrap();
        rb_tree_rotate(tree, node.parent.as_mut().unwrap(), 1 - side as i32);
    } else {
        next_node = node;
    }
    rb_tree_insert_case5(tree, next_node);
}
标准答案:
pub fn rb_tree_insert_case4<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, mut node: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    let next_node: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let side: RBTreeNodeSide;
    side = rb_tree_node_side(node);
    if side != rb_tree_node_side(node.borrow().parent) {
        next_node = node.borrow().parent;
        rb_tree_rotate(tree, node.borrow().parent, 1 - side);
    } else {
        next_node = node;
    }
    rb_tree_insert_case5(tree, next_node);
}

函数名: rb_tree_insert_case5
生成代码:
pub fn rb_tree_insert_case5<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, node: &mut RBTreeNode<K, V>) {
    let parent: &mut RBTreeNode<K, V>;
    let grandparent: &mut RBTreeNode<K, V>;
    let side: RBTreeNodeSide;

    parent = node.parent.as_mut().unwrap();
    grandparent = parent.parent.as_mut().unwrap();
    side = rb_tree_node_side(node);

    rb_tree_rotate(tree, grandparent, RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_RIGHT);
    parent.color = RBTreeNodeColor::RB_TREE_NODE_BLACK;
    grandparent.color = RBTreeNodeColor::RB_TREE_NODE_RED;
}
标准答案:
pub fn rb_tree_insert_case5<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, mut node: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>) {
    let mut parent: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut grandparent: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let side: RBTreeNodeSide;

    parent = node.borrow().parent;
    grandparent = parent.borrow().parent;
    side = rb_tree_node_side(node);
    rb_tree_rotate(tree, grandparent, 1 - side);
    parent.borrow_mut().color = rb_tree_node_black!();
    grandparent.borrow_mut().color = rb_tree_node_red!();

函数名: rb_tree_insert
生成代码:
pub fn rb_tree_insert<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, key: RBTreeKey<K>, value: RBTreeValue<V>) -> Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut node: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> = Some(Box::new(RBTreeNode::new()));
    if node.is_none() {
        return None;
    }
    if let Some(ref mut node_ref) = node {
        node_ref.key = key;
        node_ref.value = value;
        node_ref.color = RBTreeNodeColor::RB_TREE_NODE_RED;
        node_ref.children[RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize] = None;
        node_ref.children[RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize] = None;
    }
    let mut parent: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> = None;
    let mut rover: &mut Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> = &mut tree.root_node;
    while rover.is_some() {
        parent = rover.take();
        if let Some(ref parent_ref) = parent {
            if (tree.compare_func)(value, parent_ref.value) < 0 {
                rover = &mut parent_ref.children[RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize];
            } else {
                rover = &mut parent_ref.children[RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize];
            }
        }
    }
    *rover = node.take();
    if let Some(ref mut node_ref) = *rover {
        node_ref.parent = parent.take();
    }
    if let Some(ref mut node_ref) = *rover {
        rb_tree_insert_case1(tree, node_ref);
    }
    tree.num_nodes += 1;
    *rover
}
标准答案:
pub fn rb_tree_insert<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, key: RBTreeKey<K>, value: RBTreeValue<V>) -> Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut node = RBTreeNode::new();
    node.key = key;
    node.value = value;
    node.color = rb_tree_node_red!();
    node.children[0] = None;
    node.children[1] = None;
    let mut node = Link::new(node);

    let mut parent: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut rover: LinkRover<RBTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let mut side: RBTreeNodeSide;

    parent = None;
    rover = tree.root_node.rover();
    while rover.is_some() {
        parent = *rover;
        if (tree.compare_func)(&node.borrow().key, &rover.borrow().key) < 0 {
            side = rb_tree_node_left!();
        } else {
            side = rb_tree_node_right!();
        }
        rover = rover.borrow_mut().children[side as usize].rover();
    }
    *rover = node;
    node.borrow_mut().parent = parent;
    rb_tree_insert_case1(tree, node);
    tree.num_nodes += 1;
    return node;
}

函数名: rb_tree_lookup_node
生成代码:
pub fn rb_tree_lookup_node<K, V>(tree: &RBTree<K, V>, key: RBTreeKey<K>) -> Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut node = tree.root_node.clone();
    while node.is_some() {
        let node_ref = node.as_ref().unwrap();
        let diff = (tree.compare_func)(key.clone(), node_ref.key.clone());
        if diff == 0 {
            return node;
        } else if diff < 0 {
            node = node_ref.children[RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_LEFT as usize].clone();
        } else {
            node = node_ref.children[RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_RIGHT as usize].clone();
        }
    }
    None
}
标准答案:
pub fn rb_tree_lookup_node<K, V>(tree: &mut RBTree<K, V>, key: &mut RBTreeKey<K>) -> Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> {
    let mut node = tree.root_node;
    let mut side: RBTreeNodeSide;
    let mut diff: i32;
    while node.is_some() {
        diff = (tree.compare_func)(key, &node.borrow().key);
        if diff == 0 {
            return node;
        } else if diff < 0 {
            side = rb_tree_node_left!();
        } else {
            side = rb_tree_node_right!();
        }
        node = node.borrow().children[side as usize];
    }
    return None;
}

pub fn rb_tree_node_child<K, V>(node: &RBTreeNode<K, V>, side: RBTreeNodeSide) -> Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> {
    match side {
        RBTreeNodeSide::RB_TREE_NODE_LEFT => node.children[0].clone(),

标准答案:
pub fn rb_tree_node_child<K, V>(node: Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>>, side: RBTreeNodeSide) -> Link<RBTreeNode<K, V>> {
    if side == rb_tree_node_left!() || side == rb_tree_node_right!() {
        return node.borrow().children[side as usize];
    } else {
        return None;
    }
}

函数名: set_allocate_table

pub fn set_allocate_table<T>(set: &mut Set<T>) -> bool {
    if set.prime_index < set_num_primes {
        set.table_size = set_primes[set.prime_index as usize];
    } else {
        set.table_size = set.entries * 10;
    }
    set.table = vec![None; set.table_size as usize];
    !set.table.is_empty()
}
标准答案:
pub fn set_allocate_table<T>(set: &mut Set<T>) -> i32 {
    if set.prime_index < SET_NUM_PRIMES {
        set.table_size = SET_PRIMES[set.prime_index as usize];
    } else {
        set.table_size = set.entries * 10;
    }

    set.table = vec![None; set.table_size as usize];
    1
}

pub fn set_new<T>(hash_func: Option<Box<dyn Fn(SetValue<T>) -> u32>>, equal_func: Option<Box<dyn Fn(SetValue<T>, SetValue<T>) -> bool>>) -> Option<Link<Set<T>>> {
    let mut new_set = Set::new();
    new_set.hash_func = hash_func;
    new_set.equal_func = equal_func;
    new_set.entries = 0;
    new_set.prime_index = 0;
    new_set.free_func = None;
    if !set_allocate_table(&mut new_set) {
        return None;
    }
    Some(new_set)
}
标准答案:
pub fn set_new<T>(hash_func: SetHashFunc<T>, equal_func: SetEqualFunc<T>) -> Option<Set<T>> {
    let mut new_set: Set<T>;
    new_set = Set::new();
    new_set.hash_func = hash_func;
    new_set.equal_func = equal_func;
    new_set.entries = 0;
    new_set.prime_index = 0;
    new_set.free_func = |_| {};
    if set_allocate_table(&mut new_set) == 0 {
        return None;
    }
    Some(new_set)
}

函数名: set_free
生成代码:
pub fn set_free<T>(set: &mut Link<Set<T>>) {
    let set = set.as_mut().unwrap();
    for i in 0..set.table_size {
        let mut rover = set.table[i as usize].take();
        while let Some(entry) = rover {
            let next = entry.next.take();
            set_free_entry(set, Some(entry));
            rover = next;
        }
    }
    set.table.clear();
}
标准答案:
pub fn set_free<T>(set: &mut Set<T>) {
    let mut rover: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut next: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut i: u32;
    cfor!(i = 0; i < set.table_size; i += 1; {
        rover = set.table[i as usize];
        while rover.is_some() {
            next = rover.borrow().next;
            set_free_entry(set, rover);
            rover = next;
        }
    });
}

函数名: set_enlarge
生成代码:
pub fn set_enlarge<T>(set: &mut Set<T>) -> bool {
    let mut rover: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut next: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let old_table: Vec<Link<SetEntry<T>>>;
    let old_table_size: u32;
    let old_prime_index: u32;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut i: u32;

    old_table = std::mem::take(&mut set.table);
    old_table_size = set.table_size;
    old_prime_index = set.prime_index;
    set.prime_index += 1;

    if !set_allocate_table(set) {
        set.table = old_table;
        set.table_size = old_table_size;
        set.prime_index = old_prime_index;
        return false;
    }

    for i in 0..old_table_size {
        rover = old_table[i as usize];
        while rover.is_some() {
            next = rover.as_ref().unwrap().next.take();
            index = (set.hash_func.as_ref().unwrap())(rover.as_ref().unwrap().data.clone()) % set.table_size;
            rover.as_mut().unwrap().next = set.table[index as usize].take();
            set.table[index as usize] = rover;
            rover = next;
        }
    }

    drop(old_table);
    true
}
标准答案:
pub fn set_enlarge<T>(set: &mut Set<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut rover: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut next: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut old_table: Vec<Link<SetEntry<T>>>;
    let old_table_size: u32;
    let old_prime_index: u32;
    let mut index: u32;
    let mut i: u32;

    old_table = std::mem::take(&mut set.table);
    old_table_size = set.table_size;
    old_prime_index = set.prime_index;

    set.prime_index += 1;

    if set_allocate_table(set) == 0 {
        set.table = old_table;
        set.table_size = old_table_size;
        set.prime_index = old_prime_index;

        return 0;
    }

    cfor!(i = 0; i < old_table_size; i += 1; {
        rover = old_table[i as usize];
        while rover.is_some() {
            next = rover.borrow().next;
            index = (set.hash_func)(&rover.borrow().data) % set.table_size;
            rover.borrow_mut().next = set.table[index as usize];
            set.table[index as usize] = rover;
            rover = next;
        }
    });
    1
}

函数名: set_insert
生成代码:
pub fn set_insert<T>(set: &mut Set<T>, data: SetValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut newentry: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut rover: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut index: u32;

    if (set.entries * 3) / set.table_size > 0 {
        if !set_enlarge(set) {
            return 0;
        }
    }

    index = (set.hash_func.as_ref().unwrap())(data) % set.table_size;
    rover = set.table[index as usize].clone();

    while rover.is_some() {
        if (set.equal_func.as_ref().unwrap())(data, rover.as_ref().unwrap().data.clone()) {
            return 0;
        }
        rover = rover.unwrap().next.clone();
    }

    newentry = Some(Box::new(SetEntry::new()));
    if newentry.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }

    newentry.as_mut().unwrap().data = data;
    newentry.as_mut().unwrap().next = set.table[index as usize].clone();
    set.table[index as usize] = newentry;
    set.entries += 1;

    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn set_insert<T>(set: &mut Set<T>, data: SetValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut newentry: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut rover: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut index: u32;
    if (set.entries * 3) / set.table_size > 0 {
        if set_enlarge(set) == 0 {
            return 0;
        }
    }
    index = (set.hash_func)(&data) % set.table_size;
    rover = set.table[index as usize];
    
    while rover.is_some() {
        if (set.equal_func)(&data, &rover.borrow().data) != 0 {
            return 0;
        }
        rover = rover.borrow().next;
    }
    let mut newentry = SetEntry::new();
    newentry.data = data;
    newentry.next = set.table[index as usize];
    let mut newentry = Link::new(newentry);
    set.table[index as usize] = newentry;
    set.entries += 1;
    1
}

函数名: set_remove
生成代码:
pub fn set_remove<T>(set: &mut Set<T>, data: SetValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut rover: &mut Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut entry: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let index: usize;

    if let Some(hash_func) = &set.hash_func {
        index = (hash_func)(data) as usize % set.table_size as usize;
    } else {
        return 0;
    }

    rover = &mut set.table[index];

    while rover.is_some() {
        if let Some(equal_func) = &set.equal_func {
            if equal_func(data.clone(), rover.as_ref().unwrap().borrow().data.clone()) {
                entry = rover.take().unwrap();
                *rover = entry.borrow_mut().next.take();
                set.entries -= 1;
                set_free_entry(set, entry);
                return 1;
            }
        }
        rover = &mut rover.as_mut().unwrap().borrow_mut().next;
    }

    return 0;
}
标准答案:
pub fn set_remove<T>(set: &mut Set<T>, data: &mut SetValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut rover: LinkRover<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut entry: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut index: u32;
    index = (set.hash_func)(data) % set.table_size;
    rover = set.table[index as usize].rover();
    while rover.is_some() {
        if (set.equal_func)(&data, &rover.borrow().data) != 0 {
            entry = *rover;
            *rover = entry.borrow().next;
            set.entries -= 1;
            set_free_entry(set, entry);
            return 1;
        }
        rover = rover.borrow_mut().next.rover();
    }
    0
}

pub fn set_query<T>(set: &Set<T>, data: SetValue<T>) -> i32 {

    let index: u32;

    if let Some(hash_func) = &set.hash_func {
        index = hash_func(data) % set.table_size;
    } else {
        return 0;
    }

    rover = set.table[index as usize].clone();

    while rover.is_some() {
        if let Some(equal_func) = &set.equal_func {
            if equal_func(data.clone(), rover.as_ref().unwrap().data.clone()) {
                return 1;
            }
        }
        rover = rover.unwrap().next.clone();
    }

    return 0;
}

            return 1;
        }

函数名: set_to_array
生成代码:
pub fn set_to_array<T>(set: &Set<T>) -> Vec<SetValue<T>> {
    let mut array: Vec<SetValue<T>> = Vec::with_capacity(set.entries as usize);
    let mut array_counter = 0;

    for i in 0..set.table_size {
        let mut rover = set.table[i as usize].clone();
        while let Some(entry) = rover {
            array.push(entry.borrow().data.clone());
            array_counter += 1;
            rover = entry.borrow().next.clone();
        }
    }

    array
}
标准答案:
pub fn set_to_array<T: Clone>(set: &mut Set<T>) -> Vec<SetValue<T>> {
    let mut array: Vec<SetValue<T>> = vec![];
    let mut array_counter: u32 = 0;
    let mut i: u32;
    array.resize_with(set.entries as usize, || None);
    let mut rover: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    cfor!(i = 0; i < set.table_size; i += 1; {
        rover = set.table[i as usize];
        while rover.is_some() {
            array[array_counter as usize] = rover.borrow().data.clone();
            array_counter += 1;
            rover = rover.borrow().next;
        }
    });
    array
}

pub fn set_iterate<T>(set: Link<Set<T>>, iter: &mut SetIterator<T>) {
    let mut chain: u32 = 0;
    iter.set = set;
    iter.next_entry = None;
    for chain in 0..iter.set.as_ref().unwrap().table_size {
        if iter.set.as_ref().unwrap().table[chain as usize].is_some() {
            iter.next_entry = iter.set.as_ref().unwrap().table[chain as usize];
            break;
        }
    }
    iter.next_chain = chain;
}
标准答案:
pub fn set_iterate<'a, T>(set: &'a mut Set<T>, iter: &mut SetIterator<'a, T>) {
    let mut chain: u32 = 0;
    iter.set = set;
    let mut set = &mut iter.set;
    iter.next_entry = None;
    cfor!(chain = 0; chain < set.table_size; chain += 1; {
        if set.table[chain as usize].is_some() {
            iter.next_entry = set.table[chain as usize];
            break;
        }
    });
    iter.next_chain = chain;
}

函数名: set_iter_next
生成代码:
pub fn set_iter_next<T>(iterator: &mut SetIterator<T>) -> SetValue<T> {
    let set = iterator.set.as_ref().unwrap();
    if iterator.next_entry.is_none() {
        return SET_NULL;
    }
    let current_entry = iterator.next_entry.take().unwrap();
    let result = current_entry.data;
    if current_entry.next.is_some() {
        iterator.next_entry = current_entry.next.clone();
    } else {
        let mut chain = iterator.next_chain + 1;
        while chain < set.table_size {
            if set.table[chain as usize].is_some() {
                iterator.next_entry = set.table[chain as usize].clone();
                break;
            }
            chain += 1;
        }
        iterator.next_chain = chain;
    }
    result
}
标准答案:
pub fn set_iter_next<T: Clone>(iter: &mut SetIterator<T>) -> SetValue<T> {
    let set: &mut Set<T>;
    let result: SetValue<T>;
    let current_entry: Link<SetEntry<T>>;
    let mut chain: u32 = 0;
    set = iter.set;

    if iter.next_entry.is_none() {
        return None;
    }
    current_entry = iter.next_entry;
    result = current_entry.borrow().data.clone();

    if current_entry.borrow().next.is_some() {
        iter.next_entry = current_entry.borrow().next;
    } else {
        iter.next_entry = None;
        chain = iter.next_chain + 1;
        while chain < set.table_size {
            if set.table[chain as usize].is_some() {
                iter.next_entry = set.table[chain as usize];
                break;
            }
            chain += 1;
        }
        iter.next_chain = chain;
    }
    result
}

函数名: set_union
生成代码:
pub fn set_union<T>(set1: &Set<T>, set2: &Set<T>) -> Option<Link<Set<T>>> {
    let new_set = set_new(set1.hash_func.clone(), set1.equal_func.clone());
    if new_set.is_none() {
        return None;
    }
    let mut new_set = new_set.unwrap();

    let mut iterator = SetIterator::new();
    set_iterate(set1.clone(), &mut iterator);
    while set_iter_has_more(&iterator) {
        let value = set_iter_next(&mut iterator);
        if set_insert(&mut new_set, value) == 0 {
            set_free(&mut new_set);
            return None;
        }
    }

    set_iterate(set2.clone(), &mut iterator);
    while set_iter_has_more(&iterator) {
        let value = set_iter_next(&mut iterator);
        if set_query(&new_set, value) == 0 {
            if set_insert(&mut new_set, value) == 0 {
                set_free(&mut new_set);
                return None;
            }
        }
    }

    Some(new_set)
}
标准答案:
pub fn set_union<T: Clone>(set1: &mut Set<T>, set2: &mut Set<T>) -> Option<Set<T>> {
    let mut iterator: SetIterator<T> = SetIterator::new();
    let mut new_set: Set<T>;
    let mut value: SetValue<T>;

    let mut new_set = if let Some(new_set) = set_new(set1.hash_func, set1.equal_func) {
        new_set
    } else {
        return None;
    };
    set_iterate(set1, &mut iterator);
    while set_iter_has_more(&mut iterator) != 0 {
        value = set_iter_next(&mut iterator);
        if set_insert(&mut new_set, value) == 0 {
            set_free(&mut new_set);
            return None;
        }
    }
    set_iterate(set2, &mut iterator);
    while set_iter_has_more(&mut iterator) != 0 {
        value = set_iter_next(&mut iterator);
        if set_query(&mut new_set, &mut value) == 0 {
            if set_insert(&mut new_set, value) == 0 {
                set_free(&mut new_set);
                return None;
            }
        }
    }
    Some(new_set)
}

pub fn set_intersection<T>(set1: &Set<T>, set2: &Set<T>) -> Option<Link<Set<T>>> {
    let new_set = set_new(set1.hash_func.clone(), set2.equal_func.clone())?;
    let mut iterator = SetIterator::new();
    set_iterate(set1.rover(), &mut iterator);

    while set_iter_has_more(&iterator) {
        let value = set_iter_next(&mut iterator);
        if set_query(set2, value) != 0 {
            if set_insert(new_set.borrow_mut(), value) == 0 {
                set_free(&mut new_set.rover());
                return None;
            }
        }
    }

    Some(new_set)
}
标准答案:
pub fn set_intersection(set1: &mut Set<i32>, set2: &mut Set<i32>) -> Option<Set<i32>> {
    let mut iterator: SetIterator<i32> = SetIterator::new();
    let mut new_set: Set<i32>;
    let mut value: Option<i32>;

    let mut new_set = set_new(set1.hash_func, set2.equal_func);
    if new_set.is_none() {
        return None;
    }
    let mut new_set = new_set.unwrap();

    set_iterate(set1, &mut iterator);
    while set_iter_has_more(&mut iterator) != 0 {
        value = set_iter_next(&mut iterator);
        if set_query(set2, &mut value) != 0 {
            if set_insert(&mut new_set, value) == 0 {
                set_free(&mut new_set);
                return None;
            }
        }
    }
    Some(new_set)
}

函数名: slist_append
生成代码:
pub fn slist_append<T>(list: &mut Link<_SListEntry<T>>, data: SListValue<T>) -> Link<_SListEntry<T>> {
    let mut rover: Link<_SListEntry<T>>;
    let newentry: Link<_SListEntry<T>>;

    newentry = Some(Box::new(_SListEntry {
        data: data,
        next: None,
    }));

    if newentry.is_none() {
        return None;
    }

    if list.is_none() {
        *list = newentry;
    } else {
        rover = list.take();
        while rover.as_ref().unwrap().next.is_some() {
            rover = rover.unwrap().next.take();
        }
        rover.as_mut().unwrap().next = newentry;
    }

    newentry
}
标准答案:
pub fn slist_append<T>(list: &mut Link<SListEntry<T>>, data: SListValue<T>) -> Link<SListEntry<T>> {
    let mut rover: Link<SListEntry<T>>;
    let mut newentry = SListEntry::new();
    newentry.data = data;
    newentry.next = None;
    let newentry = Link::new(newentry);
    if list.is_none() {
        *list = newentry;
    } else {
        cfor!(rover = *list; rover.borrow().next.is_some(); rover = rover.borrow().next; {});
        rover.borrow_mut().next = newentry;
    }
    newentry
}

函数名: slist_to_array
生成代码:
pub fn slist_to_array<T>(list: Link<_SListEntry<T>>) -> Option<Vec<SListValue<T>>> {
    let mut rover = list;
    let length = slist_length(rover);
    let mut array = Vec::with_capacity(length);

    for _ in 0..length {
        if let Some(entry) = rover {
            array.push(entry.borrow().data);
            rover = entry.borrow().next.clone();
        } else {
            return None;
        }
    }

    Some(array)
}
标准答案:
pub fn slist_to_array<T: Clone>(list: Link<SListEntry<T>>) -> Vec<SListValue<T>> {
    let mut rover: Link<SListEntry<T>>;
    let mut array: Vec<SListValue<T>>;
    let mut length: u32;
    let mut i: u32;
    length = slist_length(list);
    array = vec![None; length as usize];
    rover = list;
    for i in 0..length {
        array[i as usize] = slist_data(rover);
        rover = slist_next(rover);
    }
    array
}

函数名: slist_remove_entry
生成代码:
pub fn slist_remove_entry<T>(list: &mut Link<_SListEntry<T>>, entry: Link<_SListEntry<T>>) -> i32 {
    if list.is_none() || entry.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    if *list == entry {
        *list = entry.as_ref().unwrap().next.clone();
    } else {
        let mut rover = list.clone();
        while rover.is_some() && rover.as_ref().unwrap().next != entry {
            rover = rover.unwrap().next.clone();
        }
        if rover.is_none() {
            return 0;
        } else {
            rover.as_mut().unwrap().next = entry.as_ref().unwrap().next.clone();
        }
    }
    drop(entry);
    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn slist_remove_entry<T>(list: &mut Link<SListEntry<T>>, mut entry: Link<SListEntry<T>>) -> i32 {
    let mut rover: Link<SListEntry<T>>;
    if list.is_none() || entry.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    if *list == entry {
        *list = entry.borrow().next;
    } else {
        rover = *list;
        while rover.is_some() && rover.borrow().next != entry {
            rover = rover.borrow().next;
        }
        if rover.is_none() {
            return 0;
        } else {
            rover.borrow_mut().next = entry.borrow().next;
        }
    }
    entry.drop();
    1
}

函数名: slist_remove_data
生成代码:
pub fn slist_remove_data<T>(list: &mut Link<_SListEntry<T>>, callback: SListEqualFunc<T>, data: SListValue<T>) -> u32 {
    let mut rover: LinkRover<_SListEntry<T>> = list.rover();
    let mut entries_removed: u32 = 0;

    while rover.is_some() {
        if callback(rover.borrow().data, data) != 0 {
            let next = rover.take().and_then(|node| node.next);
            std::mem::drop(rover.take());
            *rover = next;
            entries_removed += 1;
        } else {
            rover = rover.and_then(|node| node.next.rover());
        }
    }

    entries_removed
}
标准答案:
pub fn slist_remove_data<T>(list: &mut Link<SListEntry<T>>, callback: SListEqualFunc<T>, data: &mut SListValue<T>) -> u32 {
    let mut rover: LinkRover<SListEntry<T>>;
    let mut next: Link<SListEntry<T>>;
    let mut entries_removed = 0;
    rover = list.rover();
    while rover.is_some() {
        if callback(&rover.borrow().data, &data) != 0 {
            next = rover.borrow().next;
            rover.drop();
            *rover = next;
            entries_removed += 1;
        } else {
            rover = rover.borrow_mut().next.rover();
        }
    }
    return entries_removed;
}

函数名: slist_sort_internal
生成代码:
pub fn slist_sort_internal<T>(list: &mut Link<_SListEntry<T>>, compare_func: SListCompareFunc<T>) -> Link<_SListEntry<T>> {
    if list.is_none() || list.as_ref().unwrap().next.is_none() {
        return list.clone();
    }

    let pivot = list.take().unwrap();
    let mut less_list: Link<_SListEntry<T>> = None;
    let mut more_list: Link<_SListEntry<T>> = None;
    let mut rover = pivot.next.take();

    while let Some(mut node) = rover {
        rover = node.next.take();
        if compare_func(node.data.clone(), pivot.data.clone()) < 0 {
            node.next = less_list.take();
            less_list = Some(node);
        } else {
            node.next = more_list.take();
            more_list = Some(node);
        }
    }

    let less_list_end = slist_sort_internal(&mut less_list, compare_func);
    let more_list_end = slist_sort_internal(&mut more_list, compare_func);

    *list = less_list;
    if less_list.is_none() {
        *list = Some(pivot);
    } else {
        less_list_end.as_ref().unwrap().next = Some(pivot);
    }

    pivot.next = more_list;
    if more_list.is_none() {
        return Some(pivot);
    } else {
        return more_list_end;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn slist_sort_internal<T>(list: &mut Link<SListEntry<T>>, compare_func: SListCompareFunc<T>) -> Link<SListEntry<T>> {
    let mut pivot: Link<SListEntry<T>>;
    let mut rover: Link<SListEntry<T>>;
    let mut less_list: Link<SListEntry<T>>;
    let mut more_list: Link<SListEntry<T>>;
    let mut less_list_end: Link<SListEntry<T>>;
    let mut more_list_end: Link<SListEntry<T>>;
    
    if list.is_none() || list.borrow().next.is_none() {
        return *list;
    }

    pivot = *list;
    less_list = None;
    more_list = None;
    rover = list.borrow().next;

    while rover.is_some() {
        let next = rover.borrow().next;
        if compare_func(&rover.borrow().data, &pivot.borrow().data) < 0 {
            rover.borrow_mut().next = less_list;
            less_list = rover;
        } else {
            rover.borrow_mut().next = more_list;
            more_list = rover;
        }
        rover = next;
    }

    less_list_end = slist_sort_internal(&mut less_list, compare_func);
    more_list_end = slist_sort_internal(&mut more_list, compare_func);

    *list = less_list;
    if less_list.is_none() {
        *list = pivot;
    } else {
        less_list_end.borrow_mut().next = pivot;
    }

    pivot.borrow_mut().next = more_list;
    if more_list.is_none() {
        return pivot;
    } else {
        return more_list_end;
    }
}

pub fn slist_sort<T>(list: &mut Link<_SListEntry<T>>, compare_func: SListCompareFunc<T>) {
    slist_sort_internal(list, compare_func);
}
标准答案:
pub fn slist_sort<T>(list: &mut Link<SListEntry<T>>, compare_func: SListCompareFunc<T>) {

}

pub fn slist_find_data<T>(list: Link<_SListEntry<T>>, callback: SListEqualFunc<T>, data: SListValue<T>) -> Link<_SListEntry<T>> {
    let mut rover = list;
    while let Some(entry) = rover {
        if callback(entry.borrow().data, data) != 0 {
            return Some(entry);
        }
        rover = entry.borrow().next.clone();
    }
    None
}
标准答案:
pub fn slist_find_data<T>(list: Link<SListEntry<T>>, callback: SListEqualFunc<T>, data: &mut SListValue<T>) -> Link<SListEntry<T>> {
    let mut rover: Link<SListEntry<T>>;
    cfor!(rover = list; rover.is_some(); rover = rover.borrow().next; {
        if callback(&rover.borrow().data, &data) != 0 {
            return rover;
        }
    });
    None
}

pub fn slist_iter_has_more<T>(iter: &_SListIterator<T>) -> i32 {

        return if iter.prev_next.is_some() { 1 } else { 0 };
    } else {
        return if iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().next.is_some() { 1 } else { 0 };
    }
}

函数名: slist_iter_next
生成代码:
pub fn slist_iter_next<T>(iter: &mut _SListIterator<T>) -> SListValue<T> {
    if iter.current.is_none() || iter.current != *iter.prev_next {
        iter.current = *iter.prev_next;
    } else {
        iter.prev_next = &iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().next;
        iter.current = iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().next;
    }
    if iter.current.is_none() {
        return SLIST_NULL;
    } else {
        return iter.current.as_ref().unwrap().data;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn slist_iter_next<T: Clone>(iter: &mut SListIterator<T>) -> SListValue<T> {
    if iter.current.is_none() || iter.current != *iter.prev_next {
        iter.current = *iter.prev_next;
    } else {
        iter.prev_next = iter.prev_next.borrow_mut().next.rover();
        iter.current = iter.current.borrow().next;
    }
    if iter.current.is_none() {
        slist_null!()
    } else {
        iter.current.borrow().data.clone()
    }
}

pub fn sortedarray_first_index<T>(sortedarray: &SortedArray<T>, data: SortedArrayValue<T>, left: u32, right: u32) -> u32 {
    let mut index = left;
    let mut left = left;
    let mut right = right;

    while left < right {
        index = (left + right) / 2;
        let order = (sortedarray.cmp_func)(data, sortedarray.data[index as usize]);
        if order > 0 {
            left = index + 1;
        } else {
            right = index;
        }
    }
    index
}
标准答案:

    }

函数名: sortedarray_new
生成代码:
pub fn sortedarray_new<T>(length: u32, equ_func: SortedArrayEqualFunc<T>, cmp_func: SortedArrayCompareFunc<T>) -> Option<SortedArray<T>> {
    if equ_func.is_none() || cmp_func.is_none() {
        return None;
    }
    let length = if length == 0 { 16 } else { length };
    let data = Vec::with_capacity(length as usize);
    let sortedarray = SortedArray {
        data,
        length: 0,
        _alloced: length,
        equ_func: equ_func.unwrap(),
        cmp_func: cmp_func.unwrap(),
    };
    Some(sortedarray)
}
标准答案:
pub fn sortedarray_new<T>(mut length: u32, equ_func: SortedArrayEqualFunc<T>, cmp_func: SortedArrayCompareFunc<T>) -> Option<SortedArray<T>> {
    if length == 0 {
        length = 16;
    }

    let mut array: Vec<SortedArrayValue<T>> = vec![];
    array.resize_with(length as usize, || None);
    let mut sortedarray = SortedArray::new();
    sortedarray.data = array;
    sortedarray.length = 0;
    sortedarray._alloced = length;
    sortedarray.equ_func = equ_func;
    sortedarray.cmp_func = cmp_func;
    return Some(sortedarray);
}

函数名: sortedarray_remove_range
生成代码:
pub fn sortedarray_remove_range<T>(sortedarray: &mut SortedArray<T>, index: u32, length: u32) {
    if index >= sortedarray.length || index + length > sortedarray.length {
        return;
    }
    let src_index = index + length;
    let count = sortedarray.length - src_index;
    let src = sortedarray.data.as_mut_slice();
    let dest = &mut src[index as usize..];
    let src = &src[src_index as usize..];
    dest.copy_from_slice(src);
    sortedarray.length -= length;
}
标准答案:
pub fn sortedarray_remove_range<T>(sortedarray: &mut SortedArray<T>, index: u32, length: u32) {
    if index > sortedarray.length || index + length > sortedarray.length {
        return;
    }
    let mut i = index;
    for i in (index + length)..sortedarray.length {
        sortedarray.data[i as usize - length as usize] = sortedarray.data[i as usize].take();
    }
    sortedarray.length -= length;
}

函数名: sortedarray_insert
生成代码:
pub fn sortedarray_insert<T>(sortedarray: &mut SortedArray<T>, data: SortedArrayValue<T>) -> bool {
    let mut left: u32 = 0;
    let mut right: u32 = sortedarray.length;
    let mut index: u32 = 0;
    right = if right > 1 { right } else { 0 };

    while left != right {
        index = (left + right) / 2;
        let order = (sortedarray.cmp_func)(data, sortedarray.data[index as usize]);
        if order < 0 {
            right = index;
        } else if order > 0 {
            left = index + 1;
        } else {
            break;
        }
    }

    if sortedarray.length > 0 && (sortedarray.cmp_func)(data, sortedarray.data[index as usize]) > 0 {
        index += 1;
    }

    if sortedarray.length + 1 > sortedarray._alloced {
        let newsize = sortedarray._alloced * 2;
        sortedarray.data.resize_with(newsize as usize, || None);
        sortedarray._alloced = newsize;
    }

    sortedarray.data.insert(index as usize, data);
    sortedarray.length += 1;
    true
}
标准答案:
pub fn sortedarray_insert<T>(sortedarray: &mut SortedArray<T>, data: SortedArrayValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut left: u32 = 0;
    let mut right: u32 = sortedarray.length;
    let mut index: u32 = 0;
    right = if right > 1 { right } else { 0 };
    while left != right {
        index = (left + right) / 2;
        let order = (sortedarray.cmp_func)(&data, &sortedarray.data[index as usize]);
        if order < 0 {
            right = index;
        } else if order > 0 {
            left = index + 1;
        } else {
            break;
        }
    }
    if sortedarray.length > 0 && (sortedarray.cmp_func)(&data, &sortedarray.data[index as usize]) > 0 {
        index += 1;
    }
    if sortedarray.length + 1 > sortedarray._alloced {
        let newsize = sortedarray._alloced * 2;
        let mut data = std::mem::take(&mut sortedarray.data);
        data.resize_with(newsize as usize, || None);
        sortedarray.data = data;
        sortedarray._alloced = newsize;
    }
    for i in (index..sortedarray.length).rev() {
        sortedarray.data[i as usize + 1] = sortedarray.data[i as usize].take();
    }
    sortedarray.data[index as usize] = data;
    sortedarray.length += 1;
    return 1;
}

函数名: sortedarray_index_of
生成代码:
pub fn sortedarray_index_of<T>(sortedarray: &SortedArray<T>, data: SortedArrayValue<T>) -> i32 {
    if sortedarray.is_none() {
        return -1;
    }
    let mut left = 0;
    let mut right = sortedarray.length;
    let mut index = 0;
    right = if right > 1 { right } else { 0 };
    while left != right {
        index = (left + right) / 2;
        let order = (sortedarray.cmp_func)(data, sortedarray_get(sortedarray, index).unwrap().clone());
        if order < 0 {
            right = index;
        } else if order > 0 {
            left = index + 1;
        } else {
            left = sortedarray_first_index(sortedarray, data, left, index);
            right = sortedarray_last_index(sortedarray, data, index, right);
            for index in left..=right {
                if (sortedarray.equ_func)(data, sortedarray_get(sortedarray, index).unwrap().clone()) {
                    return index as i32;
                }
            }
            return -1;
        }
    }
    -1
}
标准答案:
pub fn sortedarray_index_of<T>(sortedarray: &mut SortedArray<T>, data: &mut SortedArrayValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut left: u32 = 0;
    let mut right: u32 = sortedarray.length;
    let mut index: u32 = 0;
    right = if right > 1 { right } else { 0 };
    while left != right {
        index = (left + right) / 2;
        let order = (sortedarray.cmp_func)(&data, &sortedarray.data[index as usize]);
        if order < 0 {
            right = index;
        } else if order > 0 {
            left = index + 1;
        } else {
            left = sortedarray_first_index(sortedarray, data, left, index);
            right = sortedarray_last_index(sortedarray, data, index, right);
            for index in left..=right {
                if (sortedarray.equ_func)(&data, &sortedarray.data[index as usize]) != 0 {
                    return index as i32;
                }
            }
            return -1;
        }
    }
    return -1;
}

函数名: sortedarray_clear

pub fn trie_new<T>() -> Option<Trie<T>> {

    new_trie.root_node = None;
    return Some(new_trie);

pub fn trie_free_list_pop(list: &mut Link<TrieNode<T>>) -> Link<TrieNode<T>> {
    let result = *list;
    if let Some(node) = result.as_mut() {
        *list = node.data.take();
    }
    result
}

    let result = *list;
    *list = result.borrow().free_data;
    return result;
}

函数名: trie_free
生成代码:
pub fn trie_free<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>) {
    let mut free_list: Link<TrieNode<T>> = None;
    let mut node: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut i: usize;

    if trie.root_node.is_some() {
        trie_free_list_push(&mut free_list, trie.root_node.take());
    }

    while free_list.is_some() {
        node = trie_free_list_pop(&mut free_list);
        for i in 0..256 {
            if node.as_ref().unwrap().next[i].is_some() {
                trie_free_list_push(&mut free_list, node.as_ref().unwrap().next[i].take());
            }
        }
        drop(node);
    }

    drop(trie);
}
标准答案:
pub fn trie_free<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>) {
    let mut free_list: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut node: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut i: i32;

    free_list = None;

    if trie.root_node.is_some() {
        trie_free_list_push(&mut free_list, trie.root_node);
    }

    while free_list.is_some() {
        node = trie_free_list_pop(&mut free_list);

        for i in 0..256 {
            if node.borrow().next[i as usize].is_some() {
                trie_free_list_push(&mut free_list, node.borrow().next[i as usize]);
            }
        }

        node.drop();
    }
}

函数名: trie_find_end
生成代码:
pub fn trie_find_end<T>(trie: &Trie<T>, key: &str) -> Link<TrieNode<T>> {
    let mut node = trie.root_node.clone();
    for ch in key.chars() {
        if node.is_none() {
            return None;
        }
        node = node.unwrap().next[ch as usize].clone();
    }
    node
}
标准答案:
pub fn trie_find_end<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &str) -> Link<TrieNode<T>> {
    let mut node = trie.root_node;
    let mut p: &str;
    cfor!(p = key; !p.is_empty(); p.next(); {
        if node.is_none() {
            return None;
        }
        node = node.borrow().next[p.current() as usize];
    });
    return node;
}

pub fn trie_find_end_binary<T>(trie: &Trie<T>, key: &[u8], key_length: usize) -> Link<TrieNode<T>> {
    let mut node = trie.root_node.clone();
    for j in 0..key_length {
        if node.is_none() {
            return None;
        }
        let c = key[j] as usize;
        node = node.unwrap().next[c].clone();
    }
    node
}

    for j in 0..key_length {
        if node.is_none() {
            return None;
        }
        let c = key[j as usize];
        node = node.borrow().next[c as usize];

函数名: trie_insert_rollback
生成代码:
pub fn trie_insert_rollback<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &[u8]) {
    let mut node: Link<TrieNode<T>> = trie.root_node.take();
    let mut prev_ptr: &mut Link<TrieNode<T>> = &mut trie.root_node;
    let mut p = key.iter();

    while let Some(current_node) = node {
        let next_index = *p.next().unwrap_or(&0) as usize;
        let next_prev_ptr = &mut current_node.next[next_index];
        let next_node = next_prev_ptr.take();
        current_node.use_count -= 1;

        if current_node.use_count == 0 {
            drop(current_node);
            *prev_ptr = None;
            *next_prev_ptr = None;
        } else {
            *prev_ptr = Some(current_node);
        }

        node = next_node;
        prev_ptr = next_prev_ptr;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn trie_insert_rollback<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &mut [u8]) {
    let mut node: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut prev_ptr: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut next_node: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut next_prev_ptr: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut p: &mut [u8];
    
    node = trie.root_node;
    prev_ptr = trie.root_node;
    p = key;

    while node.is_some() {
        next_prev_ptr = node.borrow().next[p[0] as usize];
        next_node = next_prev_ptr;
        p = &mut p[1..];
        node.borrow_mut().use_count -= 1;
        if node.borrow().use_count == 0 {
            node.drop();
            if prev_ptr.is_some() {
                prev_ptr.borrow_mut().next[p[0] as usize] = None;
            }
            next_prev_ptr = None;
        }
        node = next_node;
        prev_ptr = next_prev_ptr;
    }
}

函数名: trie_insert
生成代码:
pub fn trie_insert<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &str, value: TrieValue<T>) -> i32 {
    if value == TRIE_NULL {
        return 0;
    }

    let node = trie_find_end(trie, key);
    if node.is_some() && node.as_ref().unwrap().data != TRIE_NULL {
        node.unwrap().data = value;
        return 1;
    }

    let mut rover = &mut trie.root_node;
    let mut p = key.chars();

    loop {
        let mut node = rover.take();
        if node.is_none() {
            node = Some(Box::new(TrieNode::new()));
            if node.is_none() {
                trie_insert_rollback(trie, key.as_bytes());
                return 0;
            }
            node.as_mut().unwrap().data = TRIE_NULL;
            *rover = node;
        }

        node.as_mut().unwrap().use_count += 1;
        let c = p.next().map(|ch| ch as usize).unwrap_or(0);
        if c == 0 {
            node.as_mut().unwrap().data = value;
            break;
        }

        rover = &mut node.as_mut().unwrap().next[c];
    }

    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn trie_insert<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &str, value: TrieValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut rover: LinkRover<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut node: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut p: &str;
    let mut c: char;
    if value.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    node = trie_find_end(trie, key);
    if node.is_some() && node.borrow().data.is_some() {
        node.borrow_mut().data = value;
        return 1;
    }
    rover = trie.root_node.rover();
    p = key;
    loop {
        node = *rover;
        if node.is_none() {
            node = Link::new(TrieNode::new());
            node.borrow_mut().data = trie_null!();
            *rover = node;
        }
        node.borrow_mut().use_count += 1;
        c = p.current();
        if c == '\0' {
            node.borrow_mut().data = value;
            break;
        }
        rover = node.borrow_mut().next[c as usize].rover();
        p = &p[1..];
    }
    return 1;
}

函数名: trie_insert_binary
生成代码:
pub fn trie_insert_binary<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &[u8], key_length: usize, value: TrieValue<T>) -> i32 {
    if value == TRIE_NULL {
        return 0;
    }

    let node = trie_find_end_binary(trie, key, key_length);
    if node.is_some() && node.as_ref().unwrap().data != TRIE_NULL {
        node.unwrap().data = value;
        return 1;
    }

    let mut rover = &mut trie.root_node;
    let mut p = 0;

    loop {
        let mut node = rover.take();
        if node.is_none() {
            node = Some(Box::new(TrieNode::new()));
            if node.is_none() {
                trie_insert_rollback(trie, key);
                return 0;
            }
            node.as_mut().unwrap().data = TRIE_NULL;
        }

        node.as_mut().unwrap().use_count += 1;
        let c = key[p] as usize;

        if p == key_length {
            node.as_mut().unwrap().data = value;
            *rover = node;
            break;
        }

        rover = &mut node.as_mut().unwrap().next[c];
        p += 1;
        *rover = node;
    }

    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn trie_insert_binary<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &mut [u8], key_length: i32, value: TrieValue<T>) -> i32 {
    let mut rover: LinkRover<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut node: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut p: i32;
    let mut c: i32;
    if value.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    node = trie_find_end_binary(trie, key, key_length);
    if node.is_some() && node.borrow().data.is_some() {
        node.borrow_mut().data = value;
        return 1;
    }
    rover = trie.root_node.rover();
    p = 0;
    loop {
        node = *rover;
        if node.is_none() {
            node = Link::new(TrieNode::new());
            if node.is_none() {
                trie_insert_rollback(trie, key);
                return 0;
            }
            node.borrow_mut().data = trie_null!();
            *rover = node;
        }
        node.borrow_mut().use_count += 1;        
        if p == key_length {
            node.borrow_mut().data = value;
            break;
        }
        c = key[p as usize] as i32;
        rover = node.borrow_mut().next[c as usize].rover();
        p += 1;
    }
    return 1;
}

函数名: trie_remove_binary
生成代码:
pub fn trie_remove_binary<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &[u8], key_length: usize) -> i32 {
    let mut node: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut next: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut last_next_ptr: Option<&mut Link<TrieNode<T>>>;
    let mut p: usize;
    let mut c: u8;

    node = trie_find_end_binary(trie, key, key_length);
    if node.is_some() && node.as_ref().unwrap().data != TRIE_NULL {
        node.as_mut().unwrap().data = TRIE_NULL;
    } else {
        return 0;
    }

    node = trie.root_node.take();
    last_next_ptr = Some(&mut trie.root_node);
    p = 0;

    loop {
        c = key[p];
        next = node.as_ref().unwrap().next[c as usize].take();
        node.as_mut().unwrap().use_count -= 1;

        if node.as_ref().unwrap().use_count <= 0 {
            drop(node);
            if let Some(ptr) = last_next_ptr {
                *ptr = None;
                last_next_ptr = None;
            }
        }

        if p == key_length {
            break;
        } else {
            p += 1;
        }

        if let Some(ptr) = last_next_ptr {
            last_next_ptr = Some(&mut node.as_mut().unwrap().next[c as usize]);
        }

        node = next;
    }

    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn trie_remove_binary<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &mut [u8], key_length: i32) -> i32 {
    let mut node: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut next: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut last_next_ptr: LinkRover<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut p: i32;
    let mut c: i32;
    node = trie_find_end_binary(trie, key, key_length);
    if node.is_some() && node.borrow().data.is_some() {
        node.borrow_mut().data = trie_null!();
    } else {
        return 0;
    }
    node = trie.root_node;
    last_next_ptr = trie.root_node.rover();
    p = 0;
    loop {
        c = key[p as usize] as i32;
        next = node.borrow().next[c as usize];
        node.borrow_mut().use_count -= 1;
        if node.borrow().use_count <= 0 {
            node.drop();
            if last_next_ptr.is_some() {
                *last_next_ptr = None;
            }
        }
        if p == key_length {
            break;
        } else {
            p += 1;
        }
        if last_next_ptr.is_some() {
            last_next_ptr = last_next_ptr.borrow_mut().next[c as usize].rover();
        }
        node = next;
    }
    return 1;
}

函数名: trie_remove
生成代码:
pub fn trie_remove<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &str) -> i32 {
    let mut node: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut next: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut last_next_ptr: &mut Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut p: &str;
    let mut c: u8;

    node = trie_find_end(trie, key);
    if node.is_some() && node.as_ref().unwrap().data != TRIE_NULL {
        node.as_mut().unwrap().data = TRIE_NULL;
    } else {
        return 0;
    }

    node = trie.root_node.take();
    last_next_ptr = &mut trie.root_node;
    p = key;

    loop {
        c = p.as_bytes()[0];
        next = node.as_mut().unwrap().next[c as usize].take();
        node.as_mut().unwrap().use_count -= 1;

        if node.as_ref().unwrap().use_count <= 0 {
            drop(node);
            if last_next_ptr.is_some() {
                *last_next_ptr = None;
                last_next_ptr = &mut None;
            }
        }

        if c == b'\0' {
            break;
        } else {
            p = &p[1..];
        }

        if last_next_ptr.is_some() {
            last_next_ptr = &mut node.as_mut().unwrap().next[c as usize];
        }

        node = next;
    }

    return 1;
}
标准答案:
pub fn trie_remove<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &str) -> i32 {
    let mut node: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut next: Link<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut last_next_ptr: LinkRover<TrieNode<T>>;
    let mut p: &str;
    let mut c: char;
    node = trie_find_end(trie, key);
    if node.is_some() && node.borrow().data.is_some() {
        node.borrow_mut().data = trie_null!();
    } else {
        return 0;
    }
    node = trie.root_node;
    last_next_ptr = trie.root_node.rover();
    p = key;
    loop {
        c = p.current();
        next = node.borrow().next[c as usize];
        node.borrow_mut().use_count -= 1;
        if node.borrow().use_count <= 0 {
            node.drop();
            if last_next_ptr.is_some() {
                *last_next_ptr = None;
            }
        }
        if c == '\0' {
            break;
        } else {
            p.next();
        }
        if last_next_ptr.is_some() {
            last_next_ptr = node.borrow_mut().next[c as usize].rover();
        }
        node = next;
    }
    return 1;
}

pub fn trie_lookup<T>(trie: &Trie<T>, key: &str) -> TrieValue<T> {
    let node = trie_find_end(trie, key);
    if node.is_some() {
        return node.unwrap().borrow().data;
    } else {
        return TRIE_NULL;
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn trie_lookup<T: Clone>(trie: &mut Trie<T>, key: &str) -> TrieValue<T> {
    let node = trie_find_end(trie, key);
    if node.is_some() {
        return node.borrow().data.clone();
    } else {
        return trie_null!();
    }
}

pub fn trie_num_entries<T>(trie: &Trie<T>) -> u32 {
    if trie.root_node.is_none() {
        0
    } else {
        trie.root_node.as_ref().unwrap().use_count
    }
}
标准答案:
pub fn trie_num_entries<T>(trie: &mut Trie<T>) -> u32 {
    if trie.root_node.is_none() {
        return 0;
    } else {
        return trie.root_node.borrow().use_count;
    }
}

#[test]
fn test_arraylist_new_free() {
    let mut arraylist: Option<ArrayList<i32>>;
    arraylist = arraylist_new(0);
    assert!(arraylist.is_some());
    arraylist_free(&mut arraylist.unwrap());
    arraylist = arraylist_new(10);
    assert!(arraylist.is_some());
    arraylist_free(&mut arraylist.unwrap());
    arraylist_free(&mut None);
}

函数名: test_arraylist_append

#[test]
fn test_arraylist_append() {
    let mut arraylist: Option<ArrayList<i32>>;
    let mut i: u32;
    arraylist = arraylist_new(0);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().length, 0);
    assert_ne!(arraylist_append(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap(), Some(variable1)), 0);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().length, 1);
    assert_ne!(arraylist_append(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap(), Some(variable2)), 0);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().length, 2);
    assert_ne!(arraylist_append(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap(), Some(variable3)), 0);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().length, 3);
    assert_ne!(arraylist_append(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap(), Some(variable4)), 0);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().length, 4);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().data[0], Some(&variable1));
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().data[1], Some(&variable2));
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().data[2], Some(&variable3));
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().data[3], Some(&variable4));
    for i in 0..10000 {
        assert_ne!(arraylist_append(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap(), None), 0);
    }
    arraylist_free(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap());
    arraylist = arraylist_new(100);
}
标准答案:
fn test_arraylist_append() {
        let mut arraylist: ArrayList<i32>;
        arraylist = arraylist_new(0).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 0);

        assert_eq!(arraylist_append(&mut arraylist, Some(variable1)), 1);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 1);

        assert_eq!(arraylist_append(&mut arraylist, Some(variable2)), 1);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 2);

        assert_eq!(arraylist_append(&mut arraylist, Some(variable3)), 1);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 3);

        assert_eq!(arraylist_append(&mut arraylist, Some(variable4)), 1);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 4);

        assert_eq!(arraylist.data[0], Some(variable1));
        assert_eq!(arraylist.data[1], Some(variable2));
        assert_eq!(arraylist.data[2], Some(variable3));
        assert_eq!(arraylist.data[3], Some(variable4));

        for _ in 0..10000 {
            assert_eq!(arraylist_append(&mut arraylist, Some(0)), 1);
        }

        arraylist_free(&mut arraylist);
    }

#[test]
fn test_arraylist_prepend() {
    let mut arraylist: Option<ArrayList<i32>>;
    let mut i: i32;
    arraylist = arraylist_new(0);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().length, 0);
    assert_ne!(arraylist_prepend(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap(), Some(variable1)), 0);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().length, 1);
    assert_ne!(arraylist_prepend(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap(), Some(variable2)), 0);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().length, 2);
    assert_ne!(arraylist_prepend(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap(), Some(variable3)), 0);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().length, 3);
    assert_ne!(arraylist_prepend(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap(), Some(variable4)), 0);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().length, 4);
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().data[0], Some(&variable4));
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().data[1], Some(&variable3));
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().data[2], Some(&variable2));
    assert_eq!(arraylist.as_ref().unwrap().data[3], Some(&variable1));
    for i in 0..10000 {
        assert_ne!(arraylist_prepend(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap(), None), 0);
    }
    arraylist_free(arraylist.as_mut().unwrap());
    arraylist = arraylist_new(100);
}
标准答案:
fn test_arraylist_prepend() {
        let mut arraylist: ArrayList<i32>;
        arraylist = arraylist_new(0).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 0);

        assert_eq!(arraylist_prepend(&mut arraylist, Some(variable1)), 1);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 1);

        assert_eq!(arraylist_prepend(&mut arraylist, Some(variable2)), 1);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 2);

        assert_eq!(arraylist_prepend(&mut arraylist, Some(variable3)), 1);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 3);

        assert_eq!(arraylist_prepend(&mut arraylist, Some(variable4)), 1);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 4);

        assert_eq!(arraylist.data[0], Some(variable4));
        assert_eq!(arraylist.data[1], Some(variable3));
        assert_eq!(arraylist.data[2], Some(variable2));
        assert_eq!(arraylist.data[3], Some(variable1));

        for _ in 0..10000 {
            assert_ne!(arraylist_prepend(&mut arraylist, Some(0)), 0);
        }

        arraylist_free(&mut arraylist);
    }

函数名: test_arraylist_index_of
生成代码:
#[test]
fn test_arraylist_index_of() {
    let entries = [89, 4, 23, 42, 16, 15, 8, 99, 50, 30];
    let num_entries = entries.len();
    let mut arraylist = arraylist_new::<i32>(0).unwrap();
    let mut i;
    let mut index;
    let mut val;

    for i in 0..num_entries {
        arraylist_append(&mut arraylist, Some(entries[i]));
    }

    for i in 0..num_entries {
        val = entries[i];
        index = arraylist_index_of(&arraylist, int_equal, Some(val));
        assert_eq!(index, i as i32);
    }

    val = 0;
    assert!(arraylist_index_of(&arraylist, int_equal, Some(val)) < 0);
    val = 57;
    assert!(arraylist_index_of(&arraylist, int_equal, Some(val)) < 0);

    arraylist_free(&mut arraylist);
}
标准答案:
fn test_arraylist_index_of() {
        let entries = vec![89, 4, 23, 42, 16, 15, 8, 99, 50, 30];
        let num_entries = entries.len();
        let mut arraylist = arraylist_new(0).unwrap();

        for i in 0..num_entries {
            arraylist_append(&mut arraylist, Some(entries[i as usize]));
        }

        for i in 0..num_entries {
            let val = entries[i as usize];
            let index = arraylist_index_of(&mut arraylist, int_equal, &mut Some(val));

            assert_eq!(index, i.try_into().unwrap());
        }

        let val = 0;
        assert!(arraylist_index_of(&mut arraylist, int_equal, &mut Some(val)) < 0);
        let val = 57;
        assert!(arraylist_index_of(&mut arraylist, int_equal, &mut Some(val)) < 0);

        arraylist_free(&mut arraylist);
    }

}

函数名: test_arraylist_sort
生成代码:
#[test]
fn test_arraylist_sort() {
    let mut arraylist: Option<ArrayList<i32>>;
    let entries: [i32; 13] = [89, 4, 23, 42, 4, 16, 15, 4, 8, 99, 50, 30, 4];
    let sorted: [i32; 13] = [4, 4, 4, 4, 8, 15, 16, 23, 30, 42, 50, 89, 99];
    let num_entries: u32 = entries.len() as u32;
    let mut i: u32;

    arraylist = arraylist_new(10);
    if let Some(ref mut arraylist) = arraylist {
        for i in 0..num_entries {
            arraylist_prepend(arraylist, Some(entries[i as usize]));
        }
        arraylist_sort(arraylist, int_compare);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, num_entries);
        for i in 0..num_entries {
            let value = arraylist.data[i as usize].unwrap();
            assert_eq!(value, sorted[i as usize]);
        }
    }
    arraylist_free(&mut arraylist);

    arraylist = arraylist_new(5);
    if let Some(ref mut arraylist) = arraylist {
        arraylist_sort(arraylist, int_compare);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 0);
    }
    arraylist_free(&mut arraylist);

    arraylist = arraylist_new(5);
    if let Some(ref mut arraylist) = arraylist {
        arraylist_prepend(arraylist, Some(entries[0]));
        arraylist_sort(arraylist, int_compare);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 1);
        assert_eq!(arraylist.data[0], Some(entries[0]));
    }
    arraylist_free(&mut arraylist);
}
标准答案:
fn test_arraylist_sort() {
		let entries = vec![89, 4, 23, 42, 4, 16, 15, 4, 8, 99, 50, 30, 4];
		let sorted = vec![4, 4, 4, 4, 8, 15, 16, 23, 30, 42, 50, 89, 99];
		let num_entries = entries.len();
		let mut arraylist = arraylist_new(10).unwrap();

		for i in 0..num_entries {
			arraylist_prepend(&mut arraylist, Some(entries[i as usize]));
		}
		arraylist_sort(&mut arraylist, int_compare);
		assert_eq!(arraylist.length, num_entries.try_into().unwrap());

		for i in 0..num_entries {
			assert_eq!(arraylist.data[i as usize], Some(sorted[i as usize]));
		}
		arraylist_free(&mut arraylist);
		arraylist = arraylist_new(5).unwrap();
		arraylist_sort(&mut arraylist, int_compare);
		assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 0);
		arraylist_free(&mut arraylist);
		arraylist = arraylist_new(5).unwrap();
		arraylist_prepend(&mut arraylist, Some(entries[0]));
		arraylist_sort(&mut arraylist, int_compare);
		assert_eq!(arraylist.length, 1);
		assert_eq!(arraylist.data[0], Some(entries[0]));
		arraylist_free(&mut arraylist);
	}

函数名: find_subtree_height
生成代码:
pub fn find_subtree_height<K, V>(node: &Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) -> i32 {
    if node.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }
    let left_subtree = avl_tree_node_child(node, AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT);
    let right_subtree = avl_tree_node_child(node, AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT);
    let left_height = find_subtree_height(&left_subtree);
    let right_height = find_subtree_height(&right_subtree);
    if left_height > right_height {
        return left_height + 1;
    } else {
        return right_height + 1;
    }
}
标准答案:
fn find_subtree_height(node: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>) -> i32 {
        let left_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>;
        let right_subtree: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>;
        let left_height: i32;
        let right_height: i32;

        if node.is_none() {
            return 0;
        }

        left_subtree = avl_tree_node_child(node, avl_tree_node_left!());
        right_subtree = avl_tree_node_child(node, avl_tree_node_right!());
        left_height = find_subtree_height(left_subtree);
        right_height = find_subtree_height(right_subtree);

        if left_height > right_height {
            return left_height + 1;
        } else {
            return right_height + 1;
        }
    }

函数名: validate_subtree
生成代码:
pub fn validate_subtree<K, V>(node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>) -> i32 {
    let left_node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let right_node: Link<AVLTreeNode<K, V>>;
    let left_height: i32;
    let right_height: i32;
    let key: AVLTreeKey<K>;

    if node.is_none() {
        return 0;
    }

    let node_ref = node.as_ref().unwrap();

    left_node = avl_tree_node_child(&node, AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT);
    right_node = avl_tree_node_child(&node, AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT);

    if left_node.is_some() {
        assert_eq!(avl_tree_node_parent(&left_node), node);
    }

    if right_node.is_some() {
        assert_eq!(avl_tree_node_parent(&right_node), node);
    }

    left_height = validate_subtree(left_node);
    key = avl_tree_node_key(node_ref);
    assert!(key.unwrap() > counter);
    counter = key.unwrap();
    right_height = validate_subtree(right_node);

    assert_eq!(avl_tree_subtree_height(left_node), left_height);
    assert_eq!(avl_tree_subtree_height(right_node), right_height);
    assert!(left_height - right_height < 2 && right_height - left_height < 2);

    if left_height > right_height {
        return left_height + 1;
    } else {
        return right_height + 1;
    }
}
标准答案:
fn validate_subtree(node: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>) -> i32 {
        let left_node: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>;
        let right_node: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>;
        let left_height: i32;
        let right_height: i32;
        let key: Option<i32>;

        if node.is_none() {
            return 0;
        }

        left_node = avl_tree_node_child(node, avl_tree_node_left!());
        right_node = avl_tree_node_child(node, avl_tree_node_right!());

        if left_node.is_some() {
            assert!(avl_tree_node_parent(left_node).eq(&node));
        }
        if right_node.is_some() {
            assert!(avl_tree_node_parent(right_node).eq(&node));
        }

        left_height = validate_subtree(left_node);

        key = avl_tree_node_key(node);

        assert!(key.unwrap() > unsafe { counter });
        unsafe { counter = key.unwrap() };

        right_height = validate_subtree(right_node);

        assert_eq!(avl_tree_subtree_height(left_node), left_height);
        assert_eq!(avl_tree_subtree_height(right_node), right_height);

        assert!((left_height - right_height) < 2 && (right_height - left_height) < 2);

        if left_height > right_height {
            return left_height + 1;
        } else {
            return right_height + 1;
        }
    }

函数名: validate_tree
生成代码:
pub fn validate_tree<K, V>(tree: &AVLTree<K, V>) {
    let root_node = avl_tree_root_node(tree);
    if root_node.is_some() {
        let height = find_subtree_height(&root_node);
        assert_eq!(avl_tree_subtree_height(root_node.clone()), height);
    }
    let mut counter = -1;
    validate_subtree(root_node);
}
标准答案:
fn validate_tree(tree: &mut AVLTree<i32, i32>) {
        let root_node: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>;
        let height: i32;

        root_node = avl_tree_root_node(tree);

        if root_node.is_some() {
            height = find_subtree_height(root_node);
            assert_eq!(avl_tree_subtree_height(root_node), height);
        }

        unsafe { counter = -1; }
        validate_subtree(root_node);
    }

pub fn create_tree() -> Option<AVLTree<(), i32>> {
    let mut tree = avl_tree_new(int_compare as AVLTreeCompareFunc<i32>);
    for i in 0..NUM_TEST_VALUES {
        test_array[i] = i as i32;
        avl_tree_insert(&mut tree, Some(&test_array[i]), Some(&test_array[i]));
    }
    tree
}
标准答案:
fn create_tree() -> AVLTree<i32, i32> {
        let mut tree: AVLTree<i32, i32>;
        let mut i: i32;

        tree = avl_tree_new(int_compare).unwrap();

        for i in 0..num_test_values!() {
            unsafe {
                test_array[i as usize] = i;
            }
            avl_tree_insert(&mut tree, unsafe { Some(test_array[i as usize]) }, unsafe { Some(test_array[i as usize]) } );
        }

        return tree;
    }

函数名: test_avl_tree_insert_lookup
生成代码:
#[test]
fn test_avl_tree_insert_lookup() {
    let mut tree: AVLTree<i32, i32> = avl_tree_new(int_compare).unwrap();
    let mut node: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>;
    let mut value: Option<i32>;

    for i in 0..NUM_TEST_VALUES {
        test_array[i] = i as i32;
        avl_tree_insert(&mut tree, Some(test_array[i]), Some(test_array[i]));
        assert_eq!(avl_tree_num_entries(&tree), (i + 1) as u32);
        validate_tree(&tree);
    }

    assert!(avl_tree_root_node(&tree).is_some());

    for i in 0..NUM_TEST_VALUES {
        node = avl_tree_lookup_node(&tree, Some(i as i32));
        assert!(node.is_some());
        value = avl_tree_node_key(&node.unwrap());
        assert_eq!(value.unwrap(), i as i32);
        value = avl_tree_node_value(&node.unwrap());
        assert_eq!(value.unwrap(), i as i32);
    }

    let i = NUM_TEST_VALUES as i32 + 100;
    assert!(avl_tree_lookup_node(&tree, Some(i)).is_none());

    avl_tree_free(&mut tree);
}
标准答案:
fn test_avl_tree_insert_lookup() {
        let mut tree: AVLTree<i32, i32>;
        let mut node: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>;
        let mut i: i32;
        let mut value: Option<i32>;

        tree = avl_tree_new(int_compare).unwrap();

        for i in 0..num_test_values!() {
            unsafe {
                test_array[i as usize] = i;
            }
            avl_tree_insert(&mut tree, unsafe { Some(test_array[i as usize]) }, unsafe { Some(test_array[i as usize]) });
            assert_eq!(avl_tree_num_entries(&mut tree) as i32, i + 1);
            validate_tree(&mut tree);
        }

        for i in 0..num_test_values!() {
            node = avl_tree_lookup_node(&mut tree, &mut Some(i));
            assert!(node.is_some());
            value = avl_tree_node_key(node);
            assert_eq!(value.unwrap(), i);
            value = avl_tree_node_value(node);
            assert_eq!(value.unwrap(), i);
        }

        i = num_test_values!() + 100;
        node = avl_tree_lookup_node(&mut tree, &mut Some(i));
        assert!(node.is_none());
        validate_tree(&mut tree);
        avl_tree_free(&mut tree);

        unsafe { assert_eq!(*CREATE_COUNT.lock().unwrap(), 0) };
    }

函数名: test_avl_tree_child
生成代码:
#[test]
fn test_avl_tree_child() {
    let mut tree: AVLTree<(), i32>;
    let mut root: Link<AVLTreeNode<(), i32>>;
    let mut left: Link<AVLTreeNode<(), i32>>;
    let mut right: Link<AVLTreeNode<(), i32>>;
    let values: [i32; 3] = [1, 2, 3];
    let mut p: AVLTreeValue<i32>;
    let mut i: usize;

    tree = avl_tree_new(int_compare).unwrap();

    for i in 0..3 {
        avl_tree_insert(&mut tree, None, Some(values[i]));
    }

    root = avl_tree_root_node(&tree);
    p = avl_tree_node_value(root.as_ref().unwrap());
    assert_eq!(p.unwrap(), 2);

    left = avl_tree_node_child(&root, AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_LEFT);
    p = avl_tree_node_value(left.as_ref().unwrap());
    assert_eq!(p.unwrap(), 1);

    right = avl_tree_node_child(&root, AVLTreeNodeSide::AVL_TREE_NODE_RIGHT);
    p = avl_tree_node_value(right.as_ref().unwrap());
    assert_eq!(p.unwrap(), 3);

    assert!(avl_tree_node_child(&root, 10000).is_none());
    assert!(avl_tree_node_child(&root, 2).is_none());

    avl_tree_free(&mut tree);
}
标准答案:
fn test_avl_tree_child() {
        let mut tree: AVLTree<i32, i32>;
        let mut root: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>;
        let mut left: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>;
        let mut right: Link<AVLTreeNode<i32, i32>>;
        let values: [i32; 3] = [1, 2, 3];
        let mut p: Option<i32>;
        let mut i: i32;

        tree = avl_tree_new(int_compare).unwrap();

        for i in 0..3 {
            avl_tree_insert(&mut tree, Some(values[i]), Some(values[i]));
        }

        root = avl_tree_root_node(&mut tree);
        p = avl_tree_node_value(root);
        assert_eq!(p.unwrap(), 2);

        left = avl_tree_node_child(root, avl_tree_node_left!());
        p = avl_tree_node_value(left);
        assert_eq!(p.unwrap(), 1);

        right = avl_tree_node_child(root, avl_tree_node_right!());
        p = avl_tree_node_value(right);
        assert_eq!(p.unwrap(), 3);

        assert!(avl_tree_node_child(root, 10000).is_none());
        assert!(avl_tree_node_child(root, 2).is_none());
        validate_tree(&mut tree);
        avl_tree_free(&mut tree);

        unsafe { assert_eq!(*CREATE_COUNT.lock().unwrap(), 0) };
    }

#[test]
fn test_avl_tree_lookup() {
    let mut tree: Option<AVLTree<(), i32>> = create_tree();
    let mut value: AVLTreeValue<i32>;
    let mut i: i32;

    for i in 0..NUM_TEST_VALUES as i32 {
        value = avl_tree_lookup(&tree.unwrap(), Some(&i));
        assert!(value.is_some());
        assert_eq!(value.unwrap(), i);
    }

    i = -1;
    assert!(avl_tree_lookup(&tree.unwrap(), Some(&i)).is_none());

    i = NUM_TEST_VALUES as i32 + 1;
    assert!(avl_tree_lookup(&tree.unwrap(), Some(&i)).is_none());

    i = 8724897;
    assert!(avl_tree_lookup(&tree.unwrap(), Some(&i)).is_none());

    avl_tree_free(&mut tree.unwrap());
}

fn test_avl_tree_lookup() {

        let mut i: i32;

        value = avl_tree_lookup(&mut tree, &mut Some(i));
        assert!(value.is_none());
        i = num_test_values!() + 1;

        i = 8724897;
