函数名: free_sorted_ints
生成代码:
pub fn free_sorted_ints<T>(sortedarray: &mut SortedArray<T>)
where
    T: std::fmt::Debug,
{
    for i in 0..sortedarray_length(sortedarray) {
        if let Some(Some(pi)) = sortedarray_get(sortedarray, i) {
            drop(pi);
        }
    }
    sortedarray_free(std::mem::take(sortedarray));
}
标准答案:
fn free_sorted_ints(sortedarray: &mut SortedArray<i32>) {
        for i in 0..sortedarray_length(sortedarray) {
            let pi = sortedarray_get(sortedarray, i);
        }
        sortedarray_free(sortedarray);
    }

函数名: test_sortedarray_remove_range
生成代码:
#[test]
fn test_sortedarray_remove_range() {
    let sortedarray = generate_sortedarray();
    let mut new = [0; TEST_REMOVE_RANGE_LENGTH];
    for i in 0..TEST_REMOVE_RANGE_LENGTH {
        new[i] = *sortedarray_get(&sortedarray, (TEST_REMOVE_RANGE + TEST_REMOVE_RANGE_LENGTH + i) as u32).unwrap().unwrap();
    }
    for i in 0..TEST_REMOVE_RANGE_LENGTH {
        drop(sortedarray_get(&sortedarray, (TEST_REMOVE_RANGE + i) as u32).unwrap().unwrap());
    }
    sortedarray_remove_range(&mut sortedarray, TEST_REMOVE_RANGE as u32, TEST_REMOVE_RANGE_LENGTH as u32);
    for i in 0..TEST_REMOVE_RANGE_LENGTH {
        assert_eq!(*sortedarray_get(&sortedarray, (TEST_REMOVE_RANGE + i) as u32).unwrap().unwrap(), new[i]);
    }
    check_sorted_prop(&sortedarray);
    free_sorted_ints(&mut sortedarray);
}
标准答案:
fn test_sortedarray_remove_range() {
        let mut sortedarray = generate_sortedarray();
        let mut new = [0; test_remove_range_length!()];
        for i in 0..test_remove_range_length!() {
            new[i as usize] = sortedarray_get(&mut sortedarray, test_remove_range!() + test_remove_range_length!() + i).unwrap();
        }
        sortedarray_remove_range(&mut sortedarray, test_remove_range!(), test_remove_range_length!());
        for i in 0..test_remove_range_length!() {
            assert_eq!(sortedarray_get(&mut sortedarray, test_remove_range!() + i).unwrap(), new[i  as usize]);
        }
        check_sorted_prop(&mut sortedarray);
        free_sorted_ints(&mut sortedarray);
    }

            *sortedarray_get(&sortedarray, r as u32).unwrap().as_ref().unwrap(),

        );
    }

}

pub fn generate_trie() -> Link<Trie<i32>> {
    let mut trie = trie_new();
    let mut entries = 0;
    for i in 0..NUM_TEST_VALUES {
        test_array[i] = i as i32;
        let key = format!("{}", i);
        assert!(trie_insert(&mut trie, &key, Some(&test_array[i])) != 0);
        entries += 1;
        assert!(trie_num_entries(&trie) == entries);
    }
    trie
}
标准答案:
fn generate_trie() -> Trie<i32> {
        let mut trie = trie_new::<i32>().unwrap();
        let mut entries = 0;
        for i in 0..num_test_values!() {
            unsafe {
                test_array[i] = i as i32;
                test_strings[i] = i.to_string();
                assert_eq!(trie_insert(&mut trie, test_strings[i].as_str(), Some(test_array[i])), 1);
            }
            entries += 1;
            assert_eq!(trie_num_entries(&mut trie), entries);
        }
        return trie;
    }

函数名: test_trie_new_free
生成代码:
#[test]
fn test_trie_new_free() {
    let mut trie: Link<Trie<&str>>;
    trie = trie_new();
    assert!(trie.is_some());
    trie_free(&mut trie);
    trie = trie_new();
    assert!(trie_insert(&mut trie, "hello", Some("there")) != 0);
    assert!(trie_insert(&mut trie, "hell", Some("testing")) != 0);
    assert!(trie_insert(&mut trie, "testing", Some("testing")) != 0);
    assert!(trie_insert(&mut trie, "", Some("asfasf")) != 0);
    trie_free(&mut trie);
    trie = trie_new();
    assert!(trie_insert(&mut trie, "hello", Some("there")) != 0);
    assert!(trie_remove(&mut trie, "hello") != 0);
    trie_free(&mut trie);
}
标准答案:
fn test_trie_new_free() {
        let mut trie = trie_new::<String>().unwrap();
        trie_free(&mut trie);
        trie = trie_new::<String>().unwrap();
        assert_ne!(trie_insert(&mut trie, "hello", Some("there".to_string())), 0);
        assert_ne!(trie_insert(&mut trie, "hell", Some("testing".to_string())), 0);
        assert_ne!(trie_insert(&mut trie, "testing", Some("testing".to_string())), 0);
        assert_ne!(trie_insert(&mut trie, "", Some("asfasf".to_string())), 0);
        trie_free(&mut trie);

        unsafe { assert_eq!(*CREATE_COUNT.lock().unwrap(), 0) };
    }

函数名: test_trie_lookup

#[test]
fn test_trie_lookup() {
    let trie: Link<Trie<i32>>;
    let mut buf: [u8; 10] = [0; 10];
    let val: TrieValue<i32>;
    let i: usize;

    trie = generate_trie();
    assert_eq!(trie_lookup(&trie, "000000000000000"), TRIE_NULL);
    assert_eq!(trie_lookup(&trie, ""), TRIE_NULL);

    for i in 0..NUM_TEST_VALUES {
        write!(&mut buf[..], "{}", i).unwrap();
        val = trie_lookup(&trie, std::str::from_utf8(&buf).unwrap());
        assert_eq!(val.unwrap(), i as i32);
    }

    trie_free(&mut trie);
}
标准答案:
fn test_trie_lookup() {
        let mut trie = generate_trie();
        let mut buf = String::new();
        let mut val: Option<i32>;
        for i in 0..num_test_values!() {
            unsafe {
                buf = i.to_string();
                val = trie_lookup(&mut trie, buf.as_str());
                assert_eq!(val, Some(i as i32));
            }
        }
        trie_free(&mut trie);

        unsafe { assert_eq!(*CREATE_COUNT.lock().unwrap(), 0) };
    }

函数名: test_trie_remove
生成代码:
#[test]
fn test_trie_remove() {
    let mut trie: Link<Trie<i32>>;
    let mut buf: [u8; 10];
    let mut i: usize;
    let mut entries: u32;

    trie = generate_trie();
    assert_eq!(trie_remove(&mut trie, "000000000000000"), 0);
    assert_eq!(trie_remove(&mut trie, ""), 0);
    entries = trie_num_entries(&trie);
    assert_eq!(entries, NUM_TEST_VALUES as u32);

    for i in 0..NUM_TEST_VALUES {
        let s = format!("{}", i);
        buf.copy_from_slice(s.as_bytes());
        assert_ne!(trie_remove(&mut trie, std::str::from_utf8(&buf).unwrap()), 0);
        entries -= 1;
        assert_eq!(trie_num_entries(&trie), entries);
    }

    trie_free(&mut trie);
}
标准答案:
fn test_trie_remove() {
        let mut trie = generate_trie();
        let mut buf = String::new();
        let mut entries = trie_num_entries(&mut trie);
        assert_eq!(trie_remove(&mut trie, "000000000000000"), 0);
        assert_eq!(trie_remove(&mut trie, ""), 0);
        assert_eq!(trie_num_entries(&mut trie), entries);
        for i in 0..num_test_values!() {
            unsafe {
                buf = i.to_string();
                assert_ne!(trie_remove(&mut trie, buf.as_str()), 0);
                entries -= 1;
                assert_eq!(trie_num_entries(&mut trie), entries);
            }
        }
        trie_free(&mut trie);

        unsafe { assert_eq!(*CREATE_COUNT.lock().unwrap(), 0) };
    }

#[test]
fn test_trie_replace() {
    let mut trie: Link<Trie<i32>>;
    let val: Box<i32>;
    trie = generate_trie();

    assert!(trie_insert(&mut trie, "999", Some(*val)) != 0);
    assert!(trie_num_entries(&trie) == NUM_TEST_VALUES as u32);
    assert!(trie_lookup(&trie, "999") == Some(*val));
    drop(val);
    trie_free(&mut trie);

函数名: test_trie_insert_binary

#[test]
fn test_trie_insert_binary() {
    let mut trie: Link<Trie<&str>>;
    let value: TrieValue<&str>;
    trie = generate_binary_trie();
    assert!(trie_insert_binary(&mut trie, &bin_key, bin_key.len(), Some("hi world")) != 0);
    assert!(trie_insert_binary(&mut trie, &bin_key3, bin_key3.len(), None) == 0);
    value = trie_lookup_binary(&trie, &bin_key, bin_key.len());
    assert_eq!(value.unwrap(), "hi world");
    value = trie_lookup_binary(&trie, &bin_key2, bin_key2.len());
    assert_eq!(value.unwrap(), "goodbye world");
    trie_free(&mut trie);
}
标准答案:
fn test_trie_insert_binary() {
        let mut trie = trie_new::<i32>().unwrap();
        let mut entries = 0;
        unsafe {
            assert_ne!(trie_insert_binary(&mut trie, bin_key.as_mut(), 7, Some(1)), 0);
            entries += 1;
            assert_eq!(trie_num_entries(&mut trie), entries);
            assert_ne!(trie_insert_binary(&mut trie, bin_key2.as_mut(), 8, Some(2)), 0);
            entries += 1;
            assert_eq!(trie_num_entries(&mut trie), entries);
            assert_ne!(trie_insert_binary(&mut trie, bin_key3.as_mut(), 3, Some(3)), 0);
            entries += 1;
            assert_eq!(trie_num_entries(&mut trie), entries);
            assert_ne!(trie_insert_binary(&mut trie, bin_key4.as_mut(), 4, Some(4)), 0);
            entries += 1;
            assert_eq!(trie_num_entries(&mut trie), entries);
        }
        trie_free(&mut trie);

        unsafe { assert_eq!(*CREATE_COUNT.lock().unwrap(), 0) };
    }
